# RepKey UI consistency scan — evidence ledger

Status: 2 October 2026, source inspection only (nothing rendered or run). Companion to [the report](ui-consistency-scan-2026-10-02.md); finding IDs are stable and shared with it. Links point at the code as scanned (`main` at 4f58bbeb1): a line anchor may have drifted, and a file the implementation renamed or removed (the Portal route fallbacks, `page-states`, the image upload field and its hook) no longer resolves. References to `design-direction.md`, `pattern-spec.md` and the earlier audit mean draft notes that are not in the repository.

Legend: severity high / medium / low · visibility visible / behavioural / code-only · verdict confirmed (verified in source), adjusted (real but narrowed or downgraded by the verifier), added-by-verifier (found during verification), unverified (stated from reasoning only; flagged in the verifier note).

### FRAME-01 — Property pages sit inside a second 24px padding, and the page gutter has five independent owners

high · visible · primitive-misused · confirmed

- Authenticated main owns padding (the documented owner): main adds px-4 py-5 / md:px-6 md:py-8 for every non-full-bleed route; PageShell's doc comment says padding comes from main. Gutter 16px phone / 24px desktop. Surfaces: /properties, /portals, /notifications, /settings/_, /properties/import-google/_. — [_authenticated.tsx:254](../../../src/routes/_authenticated.tsx#L254), [page-shell.tsx:24-26](../../../src/components/layout/page-shell.tsx#L24)
- PropertyLayout adds another p-6: every route under /properties/$propertyId that is not full-bleed is wrapped in 'min-w-0 p-6' on top of main. Gutter becomes 40px phone / 48px desktop (vertical 44/56px). Surfaces: Overview, Google, Guest voice, Ratings, People, Goals (3), Property settings, Portals list, Property look, Portal group. — [$propertyId.tsx:81](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId.tsx#L81)
- Full-bleed routes drop both owners, then re-create the padding by copy-paste: Inbox, property Reviews and the Portal workspace set main to overflow-hidden with no padding; four surfaces repeat 'px-4 py-5 md:px-6 md:py-8' (or px-4 md:px-6) by hand (fallback frame, workspace body frame x2, Share tab, workspace header and tab strip). — [_authenticated.tsx:203-207](../../../src/routes/_authenticated.tsx#L203), [$propertyId.tsx:64](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId.tsx#L64), [portal-route-fallbacks.tsx:47](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-route-fallbacks.tsx#L47), [portal-workspace-body-frame.tsx:21-22](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-body-frame.tsx#L21), [portal-share.tsx:52](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-share/portal-share.tsx#L52), [portal-workspace-header.tsx:60](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-header.tsx#L60), [portal-workspace-tabs.tsx:40](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-tabs.tsx#L40)
- Router default fallbacks add a third container: default pending/error/not-found render 'page-wrap px-4 pb-8 pt-14' (max-width 72rem) inside main, and inside PropertyLayout's p-6 for property routes; different max width (1152px) and top offset (pt-14) from every PageShell tier, so a page jumps from pending to loaded. — [router.tsx:24](../../../src/router.tsx#L24), [router.tsx:57](../../../src/router.tsx#L57), [router.tsx:77](../../../src/router.tsx#L77), [styles.css:367-371](../../../src/styles.css#L367)
- Downstream component hard-codes the 16px gutter Property pages no longer have: Property settings phone nav row uses -mx-4 ... px-4 to bleed; under PropertyLayout the gutter is 40px so it bleeds 16px of 40. — [property-settings-nav.tsx:37](../../../src/components/features/property/settings/property-settings-nav.tsx#L37), [$propertyId.tsx:81](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId.tsx#L81)

**Why it matters:** Moving from Properties or Settings into any Property page shifts the content edge by 24px per side and narrows the working area, with no change of task. The shell contract (main owns padding) is violated by its own sibling layout, so every new full-bleed surface has to guess which padding to copy.

**Could be intentional:** The p-6 predates PageShell and may have been added when Property pages were the only padded surface; nothing says Property pages should be wider-gutter. The full-bleed copies are deliberate (they must own their own scroll).

**Recommended canonical:** Make main (or PageShell) the single padding owner and delete p-6 from PropertyLayout; export one 'page gutter' token/class (px-4 py-5 md:px-6 md:py-8) or a PageFrame wrapper for full-bleed surfaces instead of four string copies.

**Verifier:** Verified: _authenticated.tsx:254 main padding; $propertyId.tsx:81 adds extra p-6 on non-full-bleed; PageShell doc says main owns padding. Fallback frame copies the padding string (portal-route-fallbacks.tsx:47). Router defaults use page-wrap pt-14, a different container. The settings-nav -mx-4 consequence follows.

### FRAME-02 — Page-level loading, error and not-found have three unrelated implementations; the shared LoadingState/ErrorState are used almost only by Portal fallbacks

high · visible · divergent-implementations · confirmed

- Router defaults (applies to every route that does not override): Pending: three bare Skeletons in 'page-wrap pt-14', no aria-busy or sr-only label. Error: destructive Alert with the (prod-sanitised) message and outline 'Try again' calling router.invalidate(). Not found: neutral Alert and 'Go home' (to '/', which redirects to /login then /properties). None shows a title, breadcrumb or heading. Only the five Portal route files override these, so Properties, Overview, Google, Guest voice, Ratings, People, Goals (3), Settings, Import, Notifications, Inbox and Reviews use this. Surfaces: all non-Portal manager routes. — [router.tsx:21-32](../../../src/router.tsx#L21), [router.tsx:43-71](../../../src/router.tsx#L43), [router.tsx:74-88](../../../src/router.tsx#L74), [router.tsx:130-132](../../../src/router.tsx#L130)
- Portal fallbacks (PageShell + LoadingState/ErrorState): loading uses LoadingState inside a PageShell (dashboard-shaped skeleton: title, four KPI tiles, one chart block) even for group page and workspace. Error keeps a PageHeader with hard-coded copy ('Portals / Manage this property's public pages') and no breadcrumbs, then ErrorState (muted dashed EmptyState box, not the destructive Alert). ErrorState gets no onRetry, so no Retry and no Back. The same 'Portals' header is reused for Property look and Portal group failures (prior C8). Surfaces: Portals list (property), Portals (all properties), Property look, Portal group, Portal workspace. — [portal-route-fallbacks.tsx:9-14](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-route-fallbacks.tsx#L9), [portal-route-fallbacks.tsx:17-22](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-route-fallbacks.tsx#L17), [portal-route-fallbacks.tsx:25-37](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-route-fallbacks.tsx#L25), [portal-route-fallbacks.tsx:61-66](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-route-fallbacks.tsx#L61), [page-states.tsx:9-23](../../../src/components/layout/page-states.tsx#L9), [page-states.tsx:29-41](../../../src/components/layout/page-states.tsx#L29), [empty-state.tsx:11-23](../../../src/components/ui/empty-state.tsx#L11), [look.tsx:56-57](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/portals/look.tsx#L56), [portals/index.tsx:58-59](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/portals/index.tsx#L58), [$groupId.tsx:96-97](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/portals/groups/$groupId.tsx#L96), [portals/index.tsx:75-76](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/portals/index.tsx#L75), [$portalId.tsx:117-118](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/portals/$portalId.tsx#L117)
- Workspace fallback frame re-implements main padding: PortalFallbackFrame supplies its own scroll and px-4 py-5 md:px-6 md:py-8 around a PageShell, so a workspace failure is framed differently from a Portals list failure. — [portal-route-fallbacks.tsx:40-50](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-route-fallbacks.tsx#L40)
- Property layout's own 'not found' misuses ErrorState: 'Property not found.' ErrorState whose 'Try again' runs navigate({to:'/properties'}); not a retry, in a bespoke 'flex flex-col items-center gap-4 py-20' wrapper. — [$propertyId.tsx:69-77](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId.tsx#L69)
- People page's loading/error/forbidden states are never reachable: PeoplePage renders LoadingState, ErrorState and a LockKeyhole Alert for state loading/error/forbidden, but the route never passes state, errorMessage or onRetry (only stories do). — [people-page.tsx:84-107](../../../src/components/features/property/people/people-page.tsx#L84), [people.tsx:116-128](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/people.tsx#L116), [people-page.stories.tsx:46](../../../src/components/features/property/people/people-page.stories.tsx#L46)
- Other bespoke inline page errors: Import page builds its own destructive box (rounded-lg border-destructive/50 bg-destructive/10 role=alert) instead of Alert/FormErrorBanner; Google report uses Alert destructive; Guest voice uses neutral Alerts for 'preparing' and 'not enough evidence' where lists use EmptyState. — [import-google/index.tsx:64-80](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/import-google/index.tsx#L64), [google-performance-section.tsx:164](../../../src/components/features/property/google-performance-section.tsx#L164), [property-guest-voice-page.tsx:201-228](../../../src/components/features/property/property-guest-voice-page.tsx#L201)

**Why it matters:** On a slow or failing load the user sees a different frame, width, top offset, error colour and (non-)availability of Retry depending on section; most pages show an anonymous skeleton with no title, and Portal failures cannot be retried at all. The canonical LoadingState/ErrorState are effectively a Portal-only dialect.

**Could be intentional:** Portal fallbacks were added by a later Portal redesign round (PortalFallbackFrame comment) and the router defaults predate it; the divergence looks accidental.

**Recommended canonical:** One PageState recipe (frame + title/breadcrumb retained + destructive-Alert error with Retry + shaped skeleton with aria-busy) used as router defaults and by Portal routes; delete the Portal-only fallbacks and the unreachable People state branches.

**Verifier:** Verified: router defaults; Portal fallbacks use LoadingState/ErrorState with no onRetry and hard-coded 'Portals' header; PropertyLayout 'Property not found' uses ErrorState with a navigate as 'Try again'. PeoplePage is passed no state, so its branches are dead. High is slightly generous but the divergence is real; the 'Portal-only dialect' claim is true for route-level use.

### FRAME-03 — Loading/error fallbacks use a narrower PageShell tier than the pages they replace; sibling list pages (Goals/People vs Portals) differ in tier

medium · visible · primitive-misused · adjusted

- Documented roles: dashboard 1200 = data-dense; standard 1024 (default) = lists and management; narrow 768 = forms and settings. — [page-shell.tsx:10-14](../../../src/components/layout/page-shell.tsx#L10), [page-shell.tsx:24-30](../../../src/components/layout/page-shell.tsx#L24)
- Lists/management on dashboard in some places and standard in others: Dashboard tier: Properties list, Portals (property and all), Portal group, Property look, Overview, Google, Guest voice, Ratings. Standard tier: People list, Goals list (also a results matrix), import flow. — [property-list-page.tsx:191](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-page.tsx#L191), [portal-list-page.tsx:264](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-list-page.tsx#L264), [portal-all-properties-page.tsx:170](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-all-properties-page.tsx#L170), [people-page.tsx:73](../../../src/components/features/property/people/people-page.tsx#L73), [goals/index.tsx:109](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/index.tsx#L109), [import-google/index.tsx:53](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/import-google/index.tsx#L53)
- Forms/settings on standard, not narrow: Account settings use narrow, but Property settings, New Goal and the import flow use standard. New Goal also nests its own max-w-5xl inside a PageShell already 1024. — [settings.tsx:12](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/settings.tsx#L12), [$propertyId/settings.tsx:30](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/settings.tsx#L30), [goals/new.tsx:131](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/new.tsx#L131), [goals/new.tsx:143](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/new.tsx#L143)
- Bespoke width containers outside the tier system: Portal workspace body frame uses max-w-5xl / max-w-7xl (1280, not 1200) and the Share tab its own max-w-5xl. — [portal-workspace-body-frame.tsx:21-22](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-body-frame.tsx#L21), [portal-share.tsx:53](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-share/portal-share.tsx#L53)
- Loading/error shell narrower than the loaded page (prior C8): PortalListLoading and Portal error fallbacks wrap in default (standard 1024) PageShell, but the Portals list, Property look and group pages render at dashboard 1200, so the layout widens on load. — [portal-route-fallbacks.tsx:11](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-route-fallbacks.tsx#L11), [portal-route-fallbacks.tsx:19](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-route-fallbacks.tsx#L19), [property-look-page.tsx:95](../../../src/components/features/portal/property-look/property-look-page.tsx#L95), [portal-group-page.tsx:81](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-page.tsx#L81)

**Why it matters:** Sibling management pages (Goals vs Portals vs People) are different widths for no stated reason, and a settings-style form is 1024 in one hub and 768 in another. A page that changes width when it finishes loading reads as a glitch.

**Could be intentional:** Goals and People may have been judged less data-dense; the portal workspace bodies need to be wider than 1200 for two-column Results. The tier doc records none of those choices.

**Recommended canonical:** Keep PageShell as the single width owner: add missing tiers (or a 'wide' tier for 1280) rather than ad hoc max-w containers, state the tier rule per page type, and have every pending/error fallback take the same tier as its page.

**Verifier:** Real: PageShell default is standard, so PortalListLoading/Error stand in for dashboard-tier pages, and People and Goals list use default PageShell while Portals/Look use dashboard. Whether Goals/People are 'data-dense' is a judgement, and bespoke max-w in the workspace is justified by the full-bleed task. Keep the fallback-width mismatch as the strong part; lower the 'tiers ignored' claim to a judgement call.

### FRAME-04 — Portal route error components skip the default boundary's sanitising, reporting, sign-out redirect and retry

medium · behavioural · primitive-bypassed · confirmed

- DefaultErrorComponent (guarded): renders publicErrorMessage(error, PROD) (generic in production), reports non-expected errors with captureBrowserException, redirects to /login on a 401, offers Try again via router.invalidate(). — [router.tsx:43-71](../../../src/router.tsx#L43), [error-display.ts:16-26](../../../src/shared/security/error-display.ts#L16)
- Portal error components (unguarded): PortalListError, PortalAllPropertiesError and PortalDetailError render errorMessage(error), the raw message with no production sanitising, never call captureBrowserException, do not handle a 401 (expired session shows 'could not be loaded') and pass no onRetry. captureBrowserException is only called from router.tsx and a guest action. — [portal-route-fallbacks.tsx:21](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-route-fallbacks.tsx#L21), [portal-route-fallbacks.tsx:34](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-route-fallbacks.tsx#L34), [portal-route-fallbacks.tsx:65](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-route-fallbacks.tsx#L65)
- Blast radius: one failing read replaces the whole workspace (prior C7): the $portalId loader awaits ten ensureQueryData reads for every tab; any failure lands on PortalDetailError (no Retry, hard-coded 'Portal' header) instead of the tab that needed the read. — [$portalId.tsx:93-113](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/portals/$portalId.tsx#L93), [$portalId.tsx:118](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/portals/$portalId.tsx#L118)

**Why it matters:** The BQC-7.6 rule (no raw error text in production), Sentry capture and session-expiry handling are implemented once in the router default and silently lost on the Portal routes, which have the heaviest loaders. Failures there are neither reported nor recoverable.

**Could be intentional:** none

**Recommended canonical:** Portal routes should use the router default boundary, or the shared PageState recipe must wrap the same sanitise + capture + 401 + retry logic.

**Verifier:** Verified: Portal fallbacks call errorMessage(error) with no PROD sanitising, no captureBrowserException and no 401 handling; DefaultErrorComponent does all three. Real behavioural gap, and the raw-error-text exposure also makes it security-relevant, so medium is fair.

### FRAME-05 — Theme switching is implemented three ways with opposite cycle order

medium · behavioural · divergent-implementations · confirmed

- ThemeToggle component: outline icon button cycling light, dark, auto, labelled with the current mode. Used in public Header and as sole control on Preferences. — [theme-toggle.tsx:8-12](../../../src/components/layout/theme-toggle.tsx#L8), [theme-toggle.tsx:14-17](../../../src/components/layout/theme-toggle.tsx#L14), [header.tsx:94](../../../src/components/layout/header.tsx#L94)
- AppTopBar account-menu item: inline ternary cycles dark, light, auto (reverse order) and labels the NEXT mode with the current mode's icon. — [app-top-bar.tsx:112-124](../../../src/components/layout/app-top-bar.tsx#L112)
- Preferences page: row 'Theme' wraps ThemeToggle with Label htmlFor='theme-toggle', but the button has no such id; the setting is a single cycling icon with no visible options. — [preferences-settings-page.tsx:21-22](../../../src/components/features/settings/preferences-settings-page.tsx#L21), [theme-toggle.tsx:21-30](../../../src/components/layout/theme-toggle.tsx#L21)

**Why it matters:** Clicking 'theme' repeatedly in the app menu and in the public header/preferences walks through modes in opposite directions and shows current-vs-next labelling inconsistently.

**Could be intentional:** none

**Recommended canonical:** A single ThemeMode control (segmented or radio menu: Light / Dark / System) reused by the account menu, public header and Preferences.

**Verifier:** Verified: ThemeToggle cycles light, dark, auto (theme-toggle.tsx:9-12), AppTopBar cycles dark, light, auto and labels the next mode. Severity is more like low-medium.

### FRAME-06 — Unlayered global anchor rule overrides utility colours; navigation-like links are patched ad hoc with !important

medium · visible · missing-shared-primitive · adjusted

- The rule: a:not([data-slot=button]) ... sets color: var(--accent) outside any @layer (the only layer is @layer base at line 328), so it beats Tailwind utilities on every <a> lacking one of four data-slot values. — [styles.css:290-300](../../../src/styles.css#L290), [styles.css:328](../../../src/styles.css#L328)
- Links that rely on utility colours and therefore lose: PageHeader backTo link and BreadcrumbLink asChild are plain <a>s with no data-slot exclusion, so by cascade they render accent, not the muted/foreground written in the classes (unverified computed style). Goals list row links likewise. — [page-header.tsx:42-50](../../../src/components/layout/page-header.tsx#L42), [breadcrumb.tsx:40-52](../../../src/components/ui/breadcrumb.tsx#L40), [goals/index.tsx:143-149](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/index.tsx#L143)
- Local !important pins in 10 files: workspace tabs, workspace header pending note, portal overview cells/popovers/row actions, group menu, property list cells and row actions add text-*! / underline! to win back control. — [portal-workspace-tabs.tsx:5-9](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-tabs.tsx#L5), [portal-workspace-tabs.tsx:56-62](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-tabs.tsx#L56), [portal-workspace-header.tsx:117](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-header.tsx#L117), [property-list-cells.tsx](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-cells.tsx), [portal-overview-cells.tsx](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-cells.tsx)

**Why it matters:** Breadcrumb ancestors, back links and tab strips share one job but get their colour from three mechanisms, so some render accent and some muted; new pages will keep hitting the override.

**Could be intentional:** The comment in portal-workspace-tabs.tsx acknowledges the override; the accent default for content links is deliberate.

**Recommended canonical:** Move the anchor default into @layer base (or scope it to prose/content links) and add one NavLink/BreadcrumbLink treatment that owns active/inactive colours; remove the !important pins.

**Verifier:** Not independently verified line by line; plausible given the unlayered anchor rule and the !important pins. Claims that backTo and breadcrumb links render accent need a computed-style check, so mark the cascade consequence as unverified. The shared-primitive gap is valid.

### FRAME-07 — Permission/unavailable outcomes: /properties role denial uses /unavailable while sibling denials silently redirect; Group not-found has no action

low · visible · divergent-implementations · adjusted

- Shell-less /unavailable card on public chrome: controlled-feature gates (portals, goals, people, Google import, workspace) redirect to /unavailable, an AuthCard under the marketing Header/Footer with no sidebar, because RootDocument only drops chrome for /_authenticated and /p/$token. — [controlled-route-gate.ts:17-28](../../../src/shared/auth/controlled-route-gate.ts#L17), [unavailable.tsx:181-193](../../../src/routes/unavailable.tsx#L181), [__root.tsx:50-55](../../../src/routes/__root.tsx#L50), [goals.tsx:7](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals.tsx#L7), [people.tsx:64](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/people.tsx#L64)

low · visible · behaviour-divergence · adjusted

- description = live count/summary: Properties ('3 properties · 1 paused'), property Portals, All-properties Portals. — [property-list-page.tsx:95-100](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-page.tsx#L95), [property-list-page.tsx:195](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-page.tsx#L195), [portal-list-page.tsx:73](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-list-page.tsx#L73), [portal-list-page.tsx:270](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-list-page.tsx#L270), [portal-all-properties-page.tsx:172-181](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-all-properties-page.tsx#L172)
- description = which property you are on: Overview and Property settings put the property name there, which also appears as a breadcrumb in the same header; Ratings/Google/Guest voice/People put only a purpose sentence. — [property-overview.tsx:174-175](../../../src/components/features/property/property-overview.tsx#L174), [$propertyId/settings.tsx:32-33](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/settings.tsx#L32)
- description = static purpose sentence: Google, Guest voice, Ratings, People, Notifications, Goals, New Goal, every Account settings page. — [property-google-page.tsx:41](../../../src/components/features/property/property-google-page.tsx#L41), [property-guest-voice-page.tsx:258](../../../src/components/features/property/property-guest-voice-page.tsx#L258), [property-ratings-page.tsx:146](../../../src/components/features/property/property-ratings-page.tsx#L146), [people-page.tsx:76](../../../src/components/features/property/people/people-page.tsx#L76), [notification-page.tsx:133](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-page.tsx#L133), [goals/index.tsx:112](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/index.tsx#L112)
- description = middle-dot meta line / fallback label: Portal group 'Group · 2 portals at X · for shared results and goals'; Goal detail program.description or the filler 'Monthly Goal Program'. — [portal-group-page.tsx:78-83](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-page.tsx#L78), [$goalId.tsx:119](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/$goalId.tsx#L119)
- Title = entity name vs section name: Group page and Goal detail titled with the entity; Property home titled 'Overview', Property settings 'Property settings'; Portal workspace titles itself with the portal name in a different component. — [portal-group-page.tsx:83](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-page.tsx#L83), [$goalId.tsx:118](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/$goalId.tsx#L118), [property-overview.tsx:174](../../../src/components/features/property/property-overview.tsx#L174)
- Entity status shown in header, in a card, or nowhere: Portal workspace header shows publication status and pending note; Goal detail puts status as a Badge in the 'Program' card; a paused/archived Property shows status only in the list description and lifecycle card; Group has no status. — [portal-workspace-header.tsx:91-99](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-header.tsx#L91), [$goalId.tsx:184](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/$goalId.tsx#L184), [property-list-page.tsx:98](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-page.tsx#L98)

**Why it matters:** The first thing under the title is read as the same kind of information everywhere; here it is a count, the page's own context, or help text. Users cannot rely on the header to say 'which property/what state am I looking at'.

**Could be intentional:** Counts on list pages and purpose copy on analytics pages are plausible per-type choices; the identity-in-description and fallback-label cases look accidental.

**Recommended canonical:** Define PageHeader slots: title (what this page is, or entity name), meta (scope/identity: property name, counts, status), description (help text only). Entity-detail pages share one status placement.

**Verifier:** Slot usage varies, but count on list pages and purpose on analytics pages are plausible. Only identity-in-description duplicating the breadcrumb and the 'Monthly Goal Program' filler look accidental. Status placement is partly task-specific. Downgrade.

- Heading-looking divs with no heading element: AuthCard titles (login, join, reset, accept-invitation, /unavailable) are CardTitle divs at text-2xl. Router pending/error/not-found render no heading; EmptyState/ErrorState titles are <p>. — [auth-layout.tsx:25](../../../src/components/layout/auth-layout.tsx#L25), [card.tsx:31-38](../../../src/components/ui/card.tsx#L31), [router.tsx:21-88](../../../src/router.tsx#L21), [empty-state.tsx:17](../../../src/components/ui/empty-state.tsx#L17)
- Section headings: h2 on analytics pages, div CardTitle on settings pages: Overview/Ratings/Guest voice own h2s (text-lg); settings sections are CardTitle divs, with a handful of h2/h3 (settings 3/4, identity 0/1, organization 0/2, property settings 1/0 against 18 CardTitles). — [property-overview.tsx:188](../../../src/components/features/property/property-overview.tsx#L188), [property-ratings-page.tsx:182](../../../src/components/features/property/property-ratings-page.tsx#L182), [preferences-settings-page.tsx:15](../../../src/components/features/settings/preferences-settings-page.tsx#L15)

**Why it matters:** Screen-reader users get a different outline per surface (two h1s, zero h1s on phone Inbox and every auth/error page), and sighted users see titles at 24, 20, 16 and 14px with a display font once.

**Could be intentional:** Inbox and workspace are compact full-bleed surfaces so smaller titles are fine, but hiding the only h1 on phones and the double h1 are not.

**Recommended canonical:** Page owns exactly one h1 (PageHeader, or a compact variant keeping the element and hiding only visually); AuthCard/EmptyState take an `as` heading prop; section recipe supplies h2.

**Verifier:** Inbox h1 is text-sm inside a max-md:hidden container, so no h1 on phones, a real a11y miss. Two h1s on Organization and CardTitle-as-div auth titles are plausible. Title sizes on full-bleed surfaces are task-driven. Keep the accessibility part; the 'five sizes' claim is cosmetic.

### FRAME-11 — Global top bars: the marketing Header and the app's AppTopBar are different chromes, and unavailable/unknown-URL pages for a signed-in manager get the wrong one

low · visible · divergent-implementations · adjusted

- Header (public chrome): sticky blurred bar: wordmark pill link, 'Properties' ghost nav (hidden under sm), bell, outline icon ThemeToggle, outline 'Account' menu {Properties, Sign out} or 'Sign in'. Rendered for every route not matching /_authenticated or /p/$token: login, join, reset-password, accept-invitation, /unavailable, legal pages and (by source reasoning) unmatched URLs. — [header.tsx:80-110](../../../src/components/layout/header.tsx#L80), [__root.tsx:50-55](../../../src/routes/__root.tsx#L50), [__root.tsx:98-115](../../../src/routes/__root.tsx#L98)
- AppTopBar (manager shell): non-sticky bar: sidebar trigger, beta feedback launcher, bell, avatar-only menu {name/email, theme cycle, Sign out}. No wordmark, no nav, no ThemeToggle button. — [app-top-bar.tsx:66-142](../../../src/components/layout/app-top-bar.tsx#L66)
- AppTopBar height and trigger change by route family: h-13 (52px) on phones, but max-md:h-11 (44px) and the sidebar trigger removed at md+ whenever sidebarLocked (isFullBleed: Inbox, property Reviews, Portal workspace). The inline comment says only inbox routes compress; code applies it to three families. — [app-top-bar.tsx:59-72](../../../src/components/layout/app-top-bar.tsx#L59), [_authenticated.tsx:203-207](../../../src/routes/_authenticated.tsx#L203), [_authenticated.tsx:242](../../../src/routes/_authenticated.tsx#L242)
- Second bars inside full-bleed surfaces: Portal workspace header (px-4 md:px-6) and Inbox list header (h-14, max-md:h-11) stack under AppTopBar with different heights and padding (px-3 vs px-4/px-6). — [portal-workspace-header.tsx:60](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-header.tsx#L60), [inbox-list-header.tsx:181](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-list-header.tsx#L181)
- Sign-out and account menu differ: AppTopBar signs out then navigates to /login; the public Header signs out and stays put. Shell account menu has no Settings entry (only in sidebar footer); public one offers 'Properties'. — [app-top-bar.tsx:125-140](../../../src/components/layout/app-top-bar.tsx#L125), [__root.tsx:106-112](../../../src/routes/__root.tsx#L106), [manager-sidebar.tsx:116-128](../../../src/components/layout/manager-sidebar.tsx#L116)

**Why it matters:** A manager redirected by a capability gate, or typing a wrong URL, is thrown from the sidebar shell into a different-looking top bar with theme and account controls elsewhere; the app bar itself changes height on phones between sibling pages.

**Could be intentional:** Public vs authenticated chrome is a legitimate split, and a locked icon-rail on full-bleed routes is deliberate. The mismatch is that in-app failures route into the public one.

**Recommended canonical:** AppTopBar for any signed-in manager route (including unavailable/not-found), public Header only for signed-out pages; one shared AccountMenu component.

**Verifier:** Public Header vs AppTopBar is a legitimate split. Theme and sign-out differences are real but minor. The 'unmatched URL gets wrong chrome' claim is by source reasoning only and could not be confirmed (notFound components render inside whichever route matched). Keep only the /unavailable-in-public-chrome point (shared with finding 4) and the sidebarLocked height behaviour.

- Full-bleed predicate split across files: isWorkspaceRoute is shared (documented as the single question both layouts ask), but the Inbox/Reviews half is re-tested with pathname.includes('/reviews') in two layouts, so a new full-bleed path must be edited in both. — [_authenticated.tsx:203-207](../../../src/routes/_authenticated.tsx#L203), [$propertyId.tsx:64](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId.tsx#L64), [portal-workspace-route.ts:1-14](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-route.ts#L1)

**Why it matters:** Divergent guards hide which state a user would actually see and make adding another full-bleed surface error-prone.

**Could be intentional:** Defensive early returns are harmless in isolation.

**Recommended canonical:** One shared isFullBleedRoute(pathname) helper; one PropertyNotFound state in the layout and typed non-null property for children.

**Verifier:** The PropertyLayout ErrorState vs null in child pages is confirmed. The duplicate full-bleed predicate is confirmed (pathname.includes('/reviews') in PropertyLayout line 64 and _authenticated.tsx). The unreachable Inbox no-org state is not verified. Code-only, low.

### NAV-01 — Section navigation is rebuilt per surface: three bespoke Link lists plus a Sidebar-primitive variant and an Inbox button list, with different anatomy

medium · visible · missing-shared-primitive · confirmed

- Sidebar-primitive rows (manager sidebar and account settings sidebar): SidebarMenuButton + icon + label, no description line. Account settings adds labelled groups (You / Organization) and a 'Back to app' row. Manager sidebar is one flat list with a Dashboard category and sub-items. Surfaces: every /properties/* page, /settings/*. — [manager-sidebar.tsx:106-153](../../../src/components/layout/manager-sidebar.tsx#L106), [settings-sidebar.tsx:116-160](../../../src/components/layout/settings-sidebar.tsx#L116), [manager-nav-items.tsx:156-205](../../../src/components/layout/manager-nav-items.tsx#L156), [nav-items-shared.tsx:46-62](../../../src/components/layout/nav-items-shared.tsx#L46)
- Property settings nav (bespoke nav/ul/Link): label plus description from md up; no icon, no group headings (only a Danger zone offset). Column from md, one scroll row below. Rows 36px (min-h-9). — [property-settings-nav.tsx:36-64](../../../src/components/features/property/settings/property-settings-nav.tsx#L36), [property-settings-sections.ts:20-60](../../../src/components/features/property/settings/property-settings-sections.ts#L20), [$propertyId/settings.tsx:40-43](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/settings.tsx#L40)
- Portal editor nav (bespoke nav/ul/Link): icon + label + live value summary (lock, attention marker), two group headings, footer note, 44px rows, 288px sticky column from lg, one scroll row below. — [portal-editor-nav.tsx:38-125](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-editor-nav.tsx#L38), [portal-editor.tsx:68-76](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-editor.tsx#L68)
- Portal workspace tabs (bespoke underline strip): label-only links with 2px underline, full-width border-b strip, 44px; re-implements what ui/tabs `line` already draws. — [portal-workspace-tabs.tsx:40-62](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-tabs.tsx#L40), [tabs.tsx:27-33](../../../src/components/ui/tabs.tsx#L27)
- Inbox queue rail (ghost Button rows) and strip (pills): queues are Buttons with onClick and aria-current, not links; icon + label + count in the rail, count appended in the strip. — [inbox-queue-rail.tsx:36-119](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-queue-rail.tsx#L36), [inbox-queue-strip.tsx:68-95](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-queue-strip.tsx#L68)

**Why it matters:** Moving between Dashboard, Property settings, the Portal editor and the Inbox, the same job changes icons, descriptions, grouping, row height, active tint and mobile form. Each bespoke copy re-derives aria-current, hover, focus, scroll row and active resolver, so a fix in one never reaches the others. The owner treats the Portal editor as the reference composition (design-direction draft note), yet nothing reuses it.

**Could be intentional:** Partly. The editor carries per-section summaries and the Inbox rail carries counts. The row shell, active treatment, focus ring, heights and scroll-row mechanics have no task reason to differ.

**Recommended canonical:** Missing shared primitive: a SectionNav (list and tabs presentations, optional icon / summary / count / group heading, link-based, owns active+hover+focus and horizontal-scroll mobile form). Use portal-editor-nav.tsx as the anatomy reference and the Sidebar primitive for the app-level rail.

**Verifier:** Bespoke Link navs in property-settings-nav and portal-editor-nav confirmed, with different anatomy and row heights (min-h-9 vs min-h-11). Inbox rail and strip use Buttons. Severity high is too high: Portal editor and Inbox differ for task reasons. Medium.

### NAV-02 — Unlayered global anchor rule defeats Tailwind ink on Link-based navs; only the workspace tabs work around it with !

medium · visible · primitive-misused · adjusted

- Root cause: unlayered `a:not(...)` rule beats every Tailwind utility: excludes only data-slot button, sidebar-menu-button, sidebar-menu-sub-button and badge; outside any @layer (base starts at line 328). — [styles.css:290-299](../../../src/styles.css#L290), [styles.css:328](../../../src/styles.css#L328)
- Workspace tabs pin ink with `!` (already fixed at HEAD): hover:text-foreground!, text-foreground!, text-muted-foreground! with a comment explaining the cascade. Fixed in 4f58bbeb1 (#732), so the audit's rendered all-accent workspace tabs is stale against HEAD. — [portal-workspace-tabs.tsx:7-9](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-tabs.tsx#L7), [portal-workspace-tabs.tsx:54-57](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-tabs.tsx#L54)
- Property settings nav: both states render accent: Link carries text-foreground / text-muted-foreground / hover:text-foreground, all defeated; label span has no colour class so it inherits accent in active and inactive rows. Only bg-muted + font-medium distinguishes the active row. — [property-settings-nav.tsx:47-56](../../../src/components/features/property/settings/property-settings-nav.tsx#L47)
- Portal editor nav: Link colour classes are dead; icon turns accent: label span hard-codes text-foreground (line 108) so inactive and active labels share one ink, while the icon inherits accent in both states. — [portal-editor-nav.tsx:101-108](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-editor-nav.tsx#L101)
- Sidebar escapes the rule, then gets its own unlayered overrides that beat the primitive's classes: active row bg --accent-muted + weight 600 and accent icons set in global CSS override the cva classes in ui/sidebar.tsx. The collapsed-mode dropdown sub-item (a Link inside DropdownMenuItem) is not excluded, so it is accent when inactive and hand-mirrors the active look with bg-accent font-semibold text-(--accent). — [styles.css:338-367](../../../src/styles.css#L338), [sidebar.tsx:466](../../../src/components/ui/sidebar.tsx#L466), [nav-items-shared.tsx:171-187](../../../src/components/layout/nav-items-shared.tsx#L171)

**Why it matters:** Whether a nav link's ink is under the author's control depends on the wrapper and whether someone remembered `!`. Any new Link-based nav silently renders accent text in every state. The sidebar's look lives in global CSS rather than the primitive.

**Could be intentional:** No. The workspace tabs comment calls the cascade a problem to pin around.

**Recommended canonical:** Move nav ink into the shared nav primitive and scope the content-link rule (inside @layer base or under prose/content containers) so Link-based navs need no `!`. Keep the sidebar look inside ui/sidebar.tsx tokens rather than data-slot CSS.

**Verifier:** The unlayered a:not(...) rule is real. Workspace tabs pin ink with ! (stale vs HEAD, as stated). Property settings nav colour classes on the Link are defeated, so the label inherits accent. The editor nav label hard-codes text-foreground, so only its icon is affected. The fix is a one-line scope of the global rule. Drop the rendered-accent claims for the editor label. Medium.

The scope split is deliberate. Keep the collapse mismatch and the doubled Settings label. Drop the structural comparison.

- Route/URL-backed switching done two ways: People and Properties switch via ?tab= with Radix Tabs; Portal workspace switches via ?tab= with nav + aria-current links. — [people.tsx:126](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/people.tsx#L126), [property-list-page.tsx:208-212](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-page.tsx#L208), [portal-workspace-tabs.tsx:47-52](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-tabs.tsx#L47)

**Why it matters:** Two tab sets on adjacent screens (People and Portals) look like different products: grey pill vs underline strip. The `line` variant that would unify them has a single consumer.

**Recommended canonical:** One underline tab look for page-level views (ui/tabs line variant) with a Link-backed sibling sharing its classes; reserve the pill for in-component modes (composer, dialog).

The line variant has a single consumer and the primary vs foreground underline colour differs. The nav-landmark vs tablist split is documented and legitimate. Medium.

### NAV-06 — DESIGN.md says the sidebar becomes a sheet below 1024px but the code docks it from 768px; the property settings hub column also switches at md

low · visible · behaviour-divergence · adjusted

- Property settings: column at 768px viewport, same threshold as the 256px main sidebar: useIsMobile breakpoint 768 and Sidebar `md:block` dock the expanded sidebar at exactly the width where the 14rem hub nav becomes a column. Source-derived budget 768 - 256 - 48 - 48 - 224 - 24 = 168px for content, before card padding. Prior A2 confirmed from source. — [use-mobile.ts:3](../../../src/components/hooks/use-mobile.ts#L3), [sidebar.tsx:205](../../../src/components/ui/sidebar.tsx#L205), [_authenticated.tsx:254](../../../src/routes/_authenticated.tsx#L254), [$propertyId.tsx:81](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId.tsx#L81), [$propertyId/settings.tsx:40](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/settings.tsx#L40)
- Portal editor nav: lg (1024px): column from lg, but the route is full-bleed so the sidebar is forced to the 48px rail; nav is 288px wide. — [portal-editor-nav.tsx:40-44](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-editor-nav.tsx#L40), [portal-editor.tsx:68](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-editor.tsx#L68), [_authenticated.tsx:203-213](../../../src/routes/_authenticated.tsx#L203)
- Inbox rail vs strip: 1078px viewport via JS: computed floor (48 + 224 + 320 + 6 + 480) with a matchMedia hook rather than a CSS breakpoint. — [use-inbox-compact-layout.ts:3-10](../../../src/components/inbox/use-inbox-compact-layout.ts#L3)
- Docs and code disagree about the sidebar's mobile breakpoint: DESIGN.md says sheet below 1024px (lg); code docks from 768. — [DESIGN.md:241](../../../DESIGN.md#L241), [sidebar.tsx:205](../../../src/components/ui/sidebar.tsx#L205), [use-mobile.ts:3](../../../src/components/hooks/use-mobile.ts#L3)

**Why it matters:** The rule 'show the nav as a column once there is room' is expressed as three unrelated viewport numbers instead of available container width, so the hub nav is cramped at tablet widths while the editor nav has room.

**Could be intentional:** The Inbox floor is derived from its own panel minimums, and full-bleed routes have a different budget. The property hub's 768 is the same-threshold accident, not a decision.

**Recommended canonical:** Make the shared section-nav choose its form from container width (container query) and take sidebar state into account. Update DESIGN.md or the code so they agree.

**Verifier:** The DESIGN.md:241 vs code mismatch is confirmed. The property hub column at md matching the sidebar breakpoint is plausible but the width budget is source-derived and unverified. The Inbox JS floor is a legitimate derived value. Mostly docs drift. Low.

- Portal editor nav and workspace tabs: plain scroll rows, 44px: full-bleed rows with border-b, summaries hidden below lg; no fade, no scroll-to-active, scrollbar not hidden. — [portal-editor-nav.tsx:44](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-editor-nav.tsx#L44), [portal-editor-nav.tsx:101](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-editor-nav.tsx#L101), [portal-workspace-tabs.tsx:40-52](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-tabs.tsx#L40)
- Inbox strip: the only scroller that handles overflow: hidden scrollbar, edge fade (useStripOverflow), snap, revealActivePill; pills 32px in a 44px bar. — [inbox-queue-strip.tsx:24-60](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-queue-strip.tsx#L24), [inbox-queue-strip.tsx:68-86](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-queue-strip.tsx#L68)
- Dashboard range: Select below sm, pressed-button row from sm: a fifth idiom for 'choose one of N' on phones. — [dashboard-range-control.tsx:43-83](../../../src/components/features/dashboard/dashboard-range-control.tsx#L43)

**Why it matters:** Same task (reach a sibling section on a phone) has row heights 28, 32, 36 and 44px, and only one nav keeps the current item visible. The repo's own convention is 44px on phones (prior A7).

**Could be intentional:** The Inbox strip's extra mechanics are justified by an unbounded, count-bearing list. The other three have no reason to lack the same reveal-active and fade logic.

**Recommended canonical:** Extract the Inbox strip's overflow handling (useStripOverflow + revealActivePill + fade) into the shared nav primitive for every scroll row; one mobile row height policy; close the mobile sheet on navigation.

**Verifier:** Different mobile row heights and scroll-row mechanics confirmed (Inbox strip only one with active-pill reveal and fade). The drawer-stays-open claim is unverified: no setOpenMobile callers outside ui/sidebar.tsx, but ui/sidebar.tsx may close on click internally. Real but minor. Low.

### NAV-08 — Dashboard range control uses pressed Buttons while SegmentedControl is used elsewhere for exclusive choices

low · visible · primitive-bypassed · adjusted

- SegmentedControl: portal feature only, 9 uses: radio-group semantics, 26px segments, chip bg-card with border ring. Uses: preview language x3, preview version, print side, photo/no photo preview, background colour mode, History filter, linktree locale picker. Used as filter and value picker, never as route navigation. LinktreeLocaleTabs is named Tabs but is a SegmentedControl. — [segmented-control.tsx:1-8](../../../src/components/ui/segmented-control.tsx#L1), [segmented-control.tsx:55](../../../src/components/ui/segmented-control.tsx#L55), [segmented-control.tsx:75](../../../src/components/ui/segmented-control.tsx#L75), [portal-history-filters.tsx:1-26](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-history-filters.tsx#L1), [linktree-locale-tabs.tsx:33](../../../src/components/features/portal/link-tree/linktree-locale-tabs.tsx#L33), [portal-preview-toolbar.tsx:40](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-preview/portal-preview-toolbar.tsx#L40), [portal-preview-toolbar.tsx:54](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-preview/portal-preview-toolbar.tsx#L54)
- Dashboard range control bypasses it for the job the primitive names: SegmentedControl's header lists 'range' as a use case. DashboardRangeControl renders aria-pressed ghost/secondary Buttons at 44px (min-w-20) with a Select below sm, on three dashboard pages. — [dashboard-range-control.tsx:43-83](../../../src/components/features/dashboard/dashboard-range-control.tsx#L43), [segmented-control.tsx:2](../../../src/components/ui/segmented-control.tsx#L2), [property-ratings-page.tsx:152](../../../src/components/features/property/property-ratings-page.tsx#L152), [property-google-page.tsx:47](../../../src/components/features/property/property-google-page.tsx#L47), [property-guest-voice-page.tsx:264](../../../src/components/features/property/property-guest-voice-page.tsx#L264)
- Filters drawn as Tabs: Notifications filters are Tabs line with URL state; Properties 'Removed' view is a pill Tabs; History filter is a SegmentedControl. design-direction.md says to preserve 'measure versus filter; exclusive choice versus route navigation'. — [notification-filter-tabs.tsx:38-47](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-filter-tabs.tsx#L38), [notifications.tsx:54-55](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/notifications.tsx#L54), the design-direction draft note
- Two look-alike pills with different geometry: Tabs default h-9, p-[3px], rounded-lg, active bg-background shadow-sm; SegmentedControl p-0.5, rounded-md, 26px segments, active bg-card with a ring. Composer comment admits it is 'a segmented control by shape'. — [tabs.tsx:27](../../../src/components/ui/tabs.tsx#L27), [tabs.tsx:67](../../../src/components/ui/tabs.tsx#L67), [segmented-control.tsx:55](../../../src/components/ui/segmented-control.tsx#L55), [segmented-control.tsx:75](../../../src/components/ui/segmented-control.tsx#L75), [composer-mode-row.tsx:154-157](../../../src/components/inbox/composer-mode-row.tsx#L154)

**Why it matters:** A manager sees pill-shaped choosers at 26px, 36px and 44px with three active looks, and an 'All / Unread' filter drawn as tabs on one page and as a radio pill on another.

**Could be intentional:** Radio-group vs tablist is a genuine semantic distinction (SegmentedControl swaps no panel). Size and chip styling are not.

**Recommended canonical:** SegmentedControl for exclusive value/filter choices (add a touch size, use it for dashboard range); ui/tabs only where a panel swaps; one chip geometry shared by both.

**Verifier:** SegmentedControl is portal-feature only. The grep finds 11 JSX uses, not 9, so the count needs checking. Dashboard range uses pressed-Button groups, the bypass claimed. Radiogroup vs tablist is a legitimate semantic split. Low.

### NAV-09 — Sidebar visual active state and aria-current come from different sources (source-derived only)

low · behavioural · behaviour-divergence · adjusted

- Sidebar: visual active = hand-written regex, aria-current = TanStack prefix match: useActiveSection decides isActive from pathname regexes. The Links pass no activeOptions, and TanStack sets aria-current="page" on any prefix match (and overrides a prop value when active), so the Dashboard parent link and the Overview sub-link are aria-current on every /properties/$id/* page, while only one row is drawn active. — [manager-sidebar.tsx:26-57](../../../src/components/layout/manager-sidebar.tsx#L26), [nav-items-shared.tsx:86-92](../../../src/components/layout/nav-items-shared.tsx#L86), [nav-items-shared.tsx:171-187](../../../src/components/layout/nav-items-shared.tsx#L171), [manager-nav-items.tsx:190-205](../../../src/components/layout/manager-nav-items.tsx#L190), [link.js:37](../../../node_modules/@tanstack/react-router/dist/esm/link.js#L37), [link.js:221-223](../../../node_modules/@tanstack/react-router/dist/esm/link.js#L221)
- Four separate active resolvers: useActiveSection, useActiveSettingsSection, activePropertySettingsSection, and props-driven active in editor nav and workspace tabs; each nav passes its own aria-current. — [settings-sidebar.tsx:34-41](../../../src/components/layout/settings-sidebar.tsx#L34), [property-settings-sections.ts:66-75](../../../src/components/features/property/settings/property-settings-sections.ts#L66), [portal-editor-nav.tsx:99](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-editor-nav.tsx#L99), [portal-workspace-tabs.tsx:50](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-tabs.tsx#L50)

**Why it matters:** Assistive tech announces several 'current page' items in the main nav; visual and semantic state can drift as routes are added.

**Could be intentional:** No. Derived from source only; not verified in a browser or with a screen reader.

**Recommended canonical:** One resolver per nav owned by the shared primitive (activeOptions exact or an explicit isActive that also drives aria-current).

**Verifier:** Not browser-verified. The sidebar's multiple resolvers are real but the aria-current prefix-match claim comes from node_modules reasoning. Plausible, low.

### NAV-10 — Parent and back navigation differs per area (breadcrumbs, sidebar back row, header back button, backTo)

low · visible · divergent-implementations · adjusted

- Account settings: 'Back to app' row in the sidebar header plus a 'Settings > X' breadcrumb duplicating the active row; back goes to /properties for managers, / otherwise. — [settings-sidebar.tsx:120-127](../../../src/components/layout/settings-sidebar.tsx#L120), [members.tsx:189](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/settings/members.tsx#L189)
- Property pages: breadcrumbs, property crumb linked on some and plain on others: linked on Goals, People, Portals list, Property look, group page, settings; plain on Overview, Ratings, Google and Guest voice. — [$propertyId/settings.tsx:34-38](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/settings.tsx#L34), [goals/index.tsx:113-117](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/index.tsx#L113), [property-ratings-page.tsx:147-151](../../../src/components/features/property/property-ratings-page.tsx#L147), [property-google-page.tsx:42-46](../../../src/components/features/property/property-google-page.tsx#L42), [property-guest-voice-page.tsx:259-263](../../../src/components/features/property/property-guest-voice-page.tsx#L259)
- Portal workspace: header back button, no breadcrumb: ghost button 'Portals' (arrow-only on phone), 'Back to editing' in review. — [portal-workspace-header.tsx:56-83](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-header.tsx#L56)
- Import pages: breadcrumbs and a backTo link together: both a trail and a 'Back to properties' link to the same destination. — [import-google/index.tsx:57-61](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/import-google/index.tsx#L57), [$importId.tsx:57-61](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/import-google/$importId.tsx#L57)

**Why it matters:** Overlaps the page-header dimension, but is also secondary navigation: the same 'go up one level' is a sidebar row, a breadcrumb, a ghost button or both a trail and a link depending on area.

**Could be intentional:** Full-bleed workspaces have no room for breadcrumbs, so a header back button there is reasonable.

**Recommended canonical:** PageHeader breadcrumbs with a rule for linking the property crumb; header back button only for full-bleed surfaces.

### NAV-11 — Nav item labels, icons, counts and group headings are reused across scopes and drawn several ways

low · visible · copy-divergence · adjusted

- Icon reuse for different concepts: Users = People (sidebar) and Members (settings); Palette = Preferences and Look (editor); Layers = Open queue and portal Group; Building2 = Organization and All properties. Property settings nav has no icons while siblings do. — [manager-nav-items.tsx:79](../../../src/components/layout/manager-nav-items.tsx#L79), [settings-sidebar.tsx:60-100](../../../src/components/layout/settings-sidebar.tsx#L60), [portal-editor-section-icons.ts:17-27](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-editor-section-icons.ts#L17), [inbox-queues.ts:34](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-queues.ts#L34)
- Counts drawn four ways: Sidebar SidebarMenuBadge wrapping a destructive Badge (hidden in icon mode); Inbox rail muted tabular text (red for Escalated); Inbox strip appended text; Properties tabs inline muted tabular span. — [manager-nav-items.tsx:165-167](../../../src/components/layout/manager-nav-items.tsx#L165), [inbox-visit-badge.tsx:19-25](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-visit-badge.tsx#L19), [inbox-queue-rail.tsx:51-60](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-queue-rail.tsx#L51), [inbox-queue-strip.tsx:90](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-queue-strip.tsx#L90), [property-list-page.tsx:216-225](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-page.tsx#L216)
- Three group-label styles: SidebarGroupLabel (text-xs medium, h-8), editor nav heading (text-xs medium muted), Inbox rail heading (11px semibold uppercase tracked). — [sidebar.tsx:388-405](../../../src/components/ui/sidebar.tsx#L388), [portal-editor-nav.tsx:52](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-editor-nav.tsx#L52), [inbox-queue-rail.tsx:83](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-queue-rail.tsx#L83)

**Why it matters:** Labels like People, Profile and AI mean different things in different navs, and the user is not told which scope they are in (prior C4).

**Could be intentional:** Counts mean different things (new since last visit vs queue size), so some difference is semantic; the rendering differences are not.

**Recommended canonical:** One count slot and one group-label style in the shared nav; scope-qualified labels or a visible scope heading on each settings nav.

### COLL-01 — Sibling-view switches differ: Goals Active/History is two Buttons without selected semantics, Notifications uses line Tabs, ranges use three controls

medium · visible · primitive-bypassed · adjusted

- Tabs, default pill with count chips: Properties 'Workspace | Removed' and People 'Staff | Directory'; Properties adds tabular count spans, People none. Surfaces: /properties, /properties/$propertyId/people. — [property-list-page.tsx:208-232](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-page.tsx#L208), [people-page.tsx:99-104](../../../src/components/features/property/people/people-page.tsx#L99)
- Tabs variant=line, manual activation: Notifications filter (wraps, text-xs triggers, repeated TabsContent per option). Surfaces: /notifications. — [notification-filter-tabs.tsx:41-64](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-filter-tabs.tsx#L41)
- Two link-Buttons toggled default/outline: Goals 'Active | History' inside a div with aria-label but no role and no aria-current. Surfaces: /properties/$propertyId/goals. — [goals/index.tsx:128-146](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/index.tsx#L128)
- SegmentedControl (radiogroup) only in Portal surfaces: documented as the control for 'a short, always-answered choice ... (preview language, version, print side, range)' yet used only in Portal history/preview/look/print surfaces. — [segmented-control.tsx:1-6](../../../src/components/ui/segmented-control.tsx#L1), [portal-history-filters.tsx:19-26](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-history-filters.tsx#L19)
- Pressed-button group (>=sm) / Select (<sm) for time range: DashboardRangeControl, 44px aria-pressed ghost/secondary buttons. — [dashboard-range-control.tsx:46-86](../../../src/components/features/dashboard/dashboard-range-control.tsx#L46)
- Select for time range: Portal Results toolbar and Portal overview strip use a plain Select with different min-heights. — [portal-results-toolbar.tsx:44-66](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-analytics/portal-results-toolbar.tsx#L44), [portal-overview-results-strip.tsx:162-182](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-results-strip.tsx#L162)
- Labelled DropdownMenu radio 'Show: X' / 'Group by: X': Properties 'Show' filter and Portals 'Group by' outline buttons opening radio menus. — [property-list-toolbar.tsx:106-135](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-toolbar.tsx#L106), [portal-overview-toolbar.tsx:131-160](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-toolbar.tsx#L131)
- aria-pressed toggle buttons: Portals 'Needs attention N' outline toggle with primary tint; Properties summary strip has a different bespoke aria-pressed text button doing the same job. — [portal-overview-toolbar.tsx:114-134](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-toolbar.tsx#L114), [property-list-summary.tsx:26-44](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-summary.tsx#L26)
- Inbox: popover of five Selects (desktop), bottom sheet of choice chips (phone), queue pills/rail. — [inbox-filter-popover.tsx:50-135](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-filter-popover.tsx#L50), [inbox-filter-sheet.tsx:119-195](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-filter-sheet.tsx#L119), [inbox-choice-chips.tsx:71](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-choice-chips.tsx#L71), [inbox-queue-strip.tsx:82](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-queue-strip.tsx#L82)

**Why it matters:** A manager moving between Properties, Goals, Notifications and People meets the same task (flip between sibling views of a list) as tabs, giant buttons, underlined tabs or a dropdown. Goals' toggle also lacks the selected-state semantics Tabs/SegmentedControl give. Two purpose-built primitives exist and are bypassed.

**Could be intentional:** Partly: filtering a result set (Properties 'Show') is not switching panels (Tabs), and Inbox queues are a primary rail. But Goals Active/History is plainly a tab pair, and the time-range switch has no reason to differ.

**Recommended canonical:** ui/tabs for panel switches that change what is listed (Properties Workspace/Removed, Goals Active/History, Notifications, People) with one count-chip anatomy and one variant; ui/segmented-control for always-answered value choices including ranges; a shared ListFilter menu/toggle for narrowing filters.

**Verifier:** Goals Active/History as two Buttons (aria-label on a plain div, no aria-current) confirmed; SegmentedControl used only in Portal files (8 files) confirmed. But 'nine ways' is inflated: Properties Show filter, Portals Group-by, Needs-attention toggle and Inbox filters are narrowing filters, not sibling-view switching. The real inconsistency is Goals buttons vs Tabs, line vs default Tabs, and range switch variants. Lower to medium.

- Other kebab sizes/variants: Linktree tile kebab plain icon (36px); Inbox detail menu outline icon-sm with max-md:size-9; Portal code actions outline size-11 sm:size-9; labels 'More review actions', 'More code actions'. — [linktree-tile.tsx:170-177](../../../src/components/features/portal/link-tree/linktree-tile.tsx#L170), [inbox-detail-copy-menu.tsx:46-55](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-detail-copy-menu.tsx#L46), [portal-code-actions.tsx:44-51](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-share/portal-code-actions.tsx#L44)
- No row actions at all: Goals rows and Directory tab rows expose only a name link or nothing; template library rows embed a Switch plus an editor trigger in an 'Action' column. — [goals/index.tsx:154-182](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/index.tsx#L154), [property-reply-template-library-card.tsx:122-159](../../../src/components/features/property/property-reply-template-library-card.tsx#L122)
- Code-level: touch-size and link-ink workarounds redeclared per file: const ITEM / MENU_ITEM / BUTTON = 'min-h-11 md:min-h-8' (with 'text-foreground!' to beat the unlayered anchor colour) redeclared in 8 files under 3 names because DropdownMenuItem has no touch size and styles.css does not exempt dropdown items. — [property-list-row-actions.tsx:18](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-row-actions.tsx#L18), [portal-overview-row-actions.tsx:28-29](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-row-actions.tsx#L28), [portal-overview-property-head.tsx:34](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-property-head.tsx#L34), [portal-group-menu.tsx:23](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-menu.tsx#L23), [portal-language-menu.tsx:17](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-languages/portal-language-menu.tsx#L17), [portal-group-portals.tsx:19](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-portals.tsx#L19), [property-list-toolbar.tsx:56](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-toolbar.tsx#L56), [styles.css:290-293](../../../src/styles.css#L290)

**Why it matters:** Same job (act on one row), but a manager relearns where actions are, what they are called, whether a destructive command asks in place or sends you elsewhere, and whether the control is visible. Touch targets range from 24px to 44px.

**Could be intentional:** Property removal deliberately routes to the danger zone (documented), and notification rows are dense feed items. The label wording, trigger sizing, hover-reveal and destructive-button colouring have no such reason.

**Recommended canonical:** A shared RowActionsMenu trigger (ghost icon, size-11 md:size-8, aria-label 'More actions for {name}') plus DropdownMenuItem touch size built into ui/dropdown-menu; one rule for primary inline buttons vs kebab; one destructive confirm (variant destructive on AlertDialogAction).

**Verifier:** Kebab size/label drift is plausible, and min-h-11 md:min-h-8 appears 10 times in src (claim says 8 files, roughly fine). Several variants are justified: Properties remove routes to danger zone deliberately, notifications are dense feed rows, Members/Staff row actions are inline by task. Core real issues: inconsistent aria-label wording, trigger sizes, AlertDialogAction destructive styling. Severity high is too much.

TrendIndicator exported but has zero usages: confirmed (dead code, not inconsistency). Compact list rows vs KPI scale differences are partly intentional; keep amber token bypass as the real item.

### COLL-12 — Search, filter and sort controls differ between the Inbox list and the Properties/Portals lists (icon-only toggled vs persistent labelled controls)

low · visible · divergent-implementations · adjusted

- Inbox list header (pane toolbar): search is an icon button swapping the header for a bespoke inline Input (borderless, autofocus, Escape closes, 'N matches', separate Close X); filters an icon-only funnel (dot indicator) opening a popover of five Selects; sort a Select 'Newest/Oldest' with ArrowUpDown; on phones everything moves into a bottom Sheet with numeric badge and chips; active-filter chips appear only on phones. Surfaces: /inbox, /properties/$propertyId/reviews. — [inbox-list-header.tsx:96-101](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-list-header.tsx#L96), [inbox-list-header.tsx:127-146](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-list-header.tsx#L127), [inbox-list-search.tsx:22-47](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-list-search.tsx#L22), [inbox-filter-popover.tsx:36-47](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-filter-popover.tsx#L36), [inbox-filter-sheet.tsx:97-117](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-filter-sheet.tsx#L97), [inbox-list-panel-v2.tsx:88-95](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-list-panel-v2.tsx#L88)
- Properties and Portals toolbars: always-visible InputGroup search (type=search, w-72), labelled outline buttons 'Show: X' / 'Group by: X' / 'Sort: X' with radio menus and direction sub-group, ListFilter and ArrowDownUp glyphs, 'N of M' live count and ghost 'Clear' when narrowed; no icon-only mode, no phone sheet. Surfaces: /properties, /portals, property portals. — [property-list-toolbar.tsx:93-185](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-toolbar.tsx#L93), [portal-overview-toolbar.tsx:99-212](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-toolbar.tsx#L99)
- Glyph and vocabulary drift: Filter = lucide Filter (Inbox) vs ListFilter (Properties/Portals); sort = ArrowUpDown (Inbox, unsorted table header) vs ArrowDownUp (toolbars); sort options Newest/Oldest vs name/rating plus direction; '{n} matches' vs '{n} of {m}'. — [inbox-filter-popover.tsx:11](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-filter-popover.tsx#L11), [inbox-list-header.tsx:2](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-list-header.tsx#L2), [property-list-toolbar.tsx:5](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-toolbar.tsx#L5), [property-list-table.tsx:54](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-table.tsx#L54), [inbox-list-search.tsx:36](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-list-search.tsx#L36)
- Other lists with no search/filter: Members, Invitations, People, Goals, Notifications (tabs + property picker only) expose no search; Google import has its own. A 30-member org list has no way to find a person. — [member-table.tsx:70](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/member-table.tsx#L70), [goals/index.tsx:128-146](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/index.tsx#L128)

**Why it matters:** Search/filter/sort is the most repeated list function; the two flagship lists solve it with different controls, glyphs, active-state cues and count wording, so users cannot transfer what they learn.

**Could be intentional:** Inbox is a narrow triage pane (about 380px) and phone bar; collapsing to icons and a sheet is defensible. The glyph, count wording, sort vocabulary and clear affordances are not layout-driven.

**Recommended canonical:** The Properties/Portals toolbar vocabulary (InputGroup search with Search addon, 'Label: value' menu buttons, ListFilter/ArrowDownUp glyphs, 'N of M' + Clear) extracted as shared ListSearchField / ListFilterMenu / ListSortMenu pieces that Inbox composes in compact icon form.

**Verifier:** Inbox is a narrow triage pane with a phone sheet; icon-collapsed search/filter is a defensible layout. Real, cheap parts are only glyph (Filter vs ListFilter), 'N matches' vs 'N of M' and Clear semantics, which overlap COLL-22. Mostly intentional layout; reduce to low and narrow to glyph/wording drift.

### ACT-01 — Destructive confirm buttons are coloured four different ways, and four of them pair a token that is the same colour as the background in light mode

high · visible · primitive-bypassed · confirmed

- Primitive variant (the canonical way): AlertDialogAction accepts variant and forwards it to buttonVariants. Used for the destructive confirm in only a handful of places. Button variant destructive renders bg-destructive text-white. — [alert-dialog.tsx:132-148](../../../src/components/ui/alert-dialog.tsx#L132), [button.tsx:13-14](../../../src/components/ui/button.tsx#L13), [merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx:66](../../../src/components/features/settings/merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx#L66), [portal-disable-dialog.tsx:52](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-disable-dialog.tsx#L52), [portal-replace-code-form.tsx:118](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-share/portal-replace-code-form.tsx#L118) (conditional on kind), [leave-organization-dialog.tsx:226](../../../src/components/features/people/leave-organization-dialog.tsx#L226), [reply-message-actions.tsx:289](../../../src/components/inbox/reply-message-actions.tsx#L289), [reply-message-actions.tsx:326](../../../src/components/inbox/reply-message-actions.tsx#L326)
- Hand-spelled bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground: default (primary) variant overridden with a class string. In styles.css light mode --destructive-foreground (line 131) is oklch(0.58 0.22 25), identical to --destructive (line 50), so label and background resolve to the same colour in light mode (computed from tokens, not rendered). Dark mode foreground is near-white (line 207). The Button destructive variant uses text-white instead. — [delete-link-dialog.tsx:36](../../../src/components/features/portal/link-tree/delete-link-dialog.tsx#L36), [portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx:42](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx#L42), [portal-revoke-links-form.tsx:67](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-share/portal-revoke-links-form.tsx#L67), [portal-unsaved-changes-prompt.tsx:65](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-detail/portal-unsaved-changes-prompt.tsx#L65), [styles.css:50](../../../src/styles.css#L50), [styles.css:131](../../../src/styles.css#L131), [styles.css:207](../../../src/styles.css#L207)
- Hand-spelled bg-destructive text-white: same job, third spelling, in the Members area. — [remove-member-dialog.tsx:51](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/remove-member-dialog.tsx#L51), [invitation-table.tsx:125](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/invitation-table.tsx#L125)
- Default primary confirm after a destructive-looking trigger or menu item: Portal Archive is a red menu item (portalRowMenu flags it destructive) but its confirm is default purple primary; Archive group is red end to end. Property Remove from workspace is a solid destructive trigger but the shared ConfirmationDialog shell renders every confirm with no variant, so Remove, Archive, Restore and Disconnect all confirm identically. — [portal-row-menu.ts:55](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-row-menu.ts#L55), [portal-archive-dialog.tsx:70-92](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-archive-dialog.tsx#L70), [portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx:40-42](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx#L40), [property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx:30](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx#L30), [property-lifecycle-confirmation-dialog.tsx:51-54](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-confirmation-dialog.tsx#L51), [staff-participation-row.tsx:110-114](../../../src/components/features/staff/staff-participation-row.tsx#L110)

**Why it matters:** The same irreversible-looking commit is red in some dialogs, purple in others, and (from token values) possibly unreadable in light mode in four portal dialogs. People learn the colour as the danger signal; here it is not reliable. The fix is trivial because the primitive already has the variant.

**Could be intentional:** Archive and Restore are reversible (30-day window), so a non-red confirm for Portal Archive and Property Archive may be deliberate; Remove member and Delete link are not reversible and still differ from each other. The light-mode token collision is not intentional.

**Recommended canonical:** AlertDialogAction variant="destructive" (ui/alert-dialog.tsx). Replace all className spellings; add a tone/destructive prop to ConfirmationDialog; fix --destructive-foreground light value or stop using that token for filled buttons.

**Verifier:** Verified. styles.css:50 --destructive and :131 light --destructive-foreground are both oklch(0.58 0.22 25), so the label matches the background in light mode. Dark value at :207 is near-white. All four bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground sites exist, and two text-white spellings. AlertDialogAction does accept variant (alert-dialog.tsx:132-148). Real, and the light-mode unreadable label is a genuine bug.
