- Trigger styling: same role, five looks: solid destructive Button (Property Remove, End goal), outline + text-destructive (Members Remove, invitation Cancel), ghost muted turning red on hover (staff Archive), neutral outline (Leave organization, Archive Property, Disconnect), red menu items (Portal rows, groups, Property list), where linktree-tile bypasses the item variant with className only. — [property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx:30](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx#L30), [remove-member-dialog.tsx:29-35](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/remove-member-dialog.tsx#L29), [invitation-table.tsx:96-104](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/invitation-table.tsx#L96), [staff-participation-row.tsx:88-100](../../../src/components/features/staff/staff-participation-row.tsx#L88), [linktree-tile.tsx:183-189](../../../src/components/features/portal/link-tree/linktree-tile.tsx#L183), [portal-group-menu.tsx:107-111](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-menu.tsx#L107)

**Why it matters:** Moving between Settings, Portals, Property and Goals, the same class of action asks for confirmation in one place, asks nothing in the next, and looks different each time. Organization Disconnect Google is the sharpest case: higher blast radius than the Property-level disconnect that is confirmed.

**Could be intentional:** Low-blast-radius or easily-undone actions (remove a language from a draft, delete an unsent draft, dismiss a notification) may reasonably skip confirmation; the Inbox reject reason field is a genuine input step. End goal and org Google disconnect are not obviously in that class (reversibility of End goal not verified).

**Recommended canonical:** AlertDialog + destructive AlertDialogAction as the one confirm pattern (extract the ConfirmationDialog shell from property-lifecycle-confirmation-dialog.tsx into ui/ with a tone prop); a written policy for which actions may skip it.

**Verifier:** Core claims hold: google-connection-settings-row:93 calls onDisconnect directly, integrations-settings-page:52-58 only toasts on failure; goals/$goalId:148-175 End goal is destructive with no confirm and the same button is duplicated across both ternary branches. But 'five mechanisms' is inflated: the inline Reject panel, Leave-org Dialog and restore panel are task-driven. Low-blast items are plausibly fine. Trigger-styling sub-list partly duplicates ACT-01/ACT-05. Reversibility of End goal unverified.

### ACT-03 — No density mechanism in Button: per-file min-h-11/md:min-h-8 constants and an undocumented 36 vs 44 split

medium · visible · missing-shared-primitive · adjusted

- Portal/Property/Settings-AI: 44 px on touch: recipe min-h-11 sm:min-h-9 (default size), min-h-11 md:min-h-8 (sm), size-11 md:size-8 (icon), with w-full sm:w-auto on stacked actions. 31 of 109 Buttons under features/portal carry it; sm: and md: used interchangeably, even between siblings in one header (portal-workspace-header.tsx:62 sm:min-h-8 vs :140 sm:min-h-9). — [portal-list-page.tsx:231](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-list-page.tsx#L231), [portal-group-goals.tsx:51](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-goals.tsx#L51), [portal-overview-row-actions.tsx:28-29](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-row-actions.tsx#L28), [portal-workspace-header.tsx:62](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-workspace/portal-workspace-header.tsx#L62), [merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx:46](../../../src/components/features/settings/merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx#L46), [reply-template-editor.tsx:89](../../../src/components/features/property/reply-template-editor.tsx#L89), [property-reply-profile-card.tsx:192](../../../src/components/features/property/property-reply-profile-card.tsx#L192)
- Inbox/notification/top bar: deliberately 36 px on touch: max-md:h-9 / max-md:size-9 / max-md:min-h-9 (26 of 59 Buttons under inbox). Comments record that an earlier round raised these to 44 and a later one lowered them to 36 on purpose, so the two areas are documented as opposite policies. — [reply-message-actions.tsx:36-50](../../../src/components/inbox/reply-message-actions.tsx#L36), [reply-editor-views.tsx:182-193](../../../src/components/inbox/reply-editor-views.tsx#L182), [reply-composer-footer.tsx:76](../../../src/components/inbox/reply-composer-footer.tsx#L76), [inbox-detail-header.tsx:78](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-detail-header.tsx#L78), [notification-panel.tsx:183](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-panel.tsx#L183), [app-top-bar.tsx:90](../../../src/components/layout/app-top-bar.tsx#L90), [beta-feedback-launcher.tsx:81](../../../src/components/features/beta-feedback/beta-feedback-launcher.tsx#L81)
- Unconditional 44 px (also on desktop): h-11 / size-11 with no breakpoint, so the control is taller than every neighbour on desktop. — [dashboard-range-control.tsx:70](../../../src/components/features/dashboard/dashboard-range-control.tsx#L70), [overview-guest-voice.tsx:59](../../../src/components/features/property/overview-guest-voice.tsx#L59), [property-overview.tsx:209](../../../src/components/features/property/property-overview.tsx#L209), [google-performance-section.tsx:130](../../../src/components/features/property/google-performance-section.tsx#L130), [inbox-filter-sheet.tsx:194](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-filter-sheet.tsx#L194) (size lg overridden to h-11)
- Untouched 36/32 px default: Settings forms (Profile, Security, Organization, most Property cards), dialogs and AlertDialog footers keep ui/button.tsx defaults (h-9 / sm h-8 / icon size-9), so Save buttons are 36 px beside 44 px Save buttons in two Property reply cards. — [button.tsx:21-30](../../../src/components/ui/button.tsx#L21), [profile-settings-form.tsx:119-127](../../../src/components/features/identity/profile-settings-form.tsx#L119), [property-profile-card.tsx:151-155](../../../src/components/features/property/settings/property-profile-card.tsx#L151)
- size="sm" silently overridden to the default height: toolbar triggers use size sm then CONTROL_HEIGHT h-11 md:h-9 (identical constant defined twice), so sm is 36 px on desktop in toolbars but 32 px elsewhere. — [property-list-toolbar.tsx:55](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-toolbar.tsx#L55), [portal-overview-toolbar.tsx:53](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-toolbar.tsx#L53), [portal-overview-results-strip.tsx:135](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-results-strip.tsx#L135)
- Per-file constants for the same recipe: ITEM / MENU_ITEM / BUTTON = min-h-11 md:min-h-8 in nine files; CONTROL_HEIGHT h-11 md:h-9 in two; TOUCH max-md:h-9 in one. — [portal-overview-row-actions.tsx:28-29](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-row-actions.tsx#L28), [portal-group-menu.tsx:23](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-menu.tsx#L23), [portal-language-menu.tsx:17](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-languages/portal-language-menu.tsx#L17), [portal-overview-property-head.tsx:34](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-property-head.tsx#L34), [property-list-row-actions.tsx:18](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-row-actions.tsx#L18), [property-list-toolbar.tsx:55-56](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-toolbar.tsx#L55), [portal-overview-toolbar.tsx:53-54](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-toolbar.tsx#L53), [portal-group-portals.tsx:19](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-portals.tsx#L19), [portal-all-properties-new-portal.tsx:19](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-all-properties-new-portal.tsx#L19), [reply-message-actions.tsx:50](../../../src/components/inbox/reply-message-actions.tsx#L50)

**Why it matters:** Phone users get 44 px targets in Portals and 36 px in the Inbox reply dock for the same Confirm/Cancel/Save job. Because Button has no density API, each file re-derives numbers and breakpoint (sm vs md), so drift is guaranteed and Settings remains at 36.

**Could be intentional:** Inbox 36 px is an explicit documented decision (dense triage workspace, WCAG 2.5.8 AA minimum is 24 px); Portal 44 px follows the stricter AAA target. The finding is that no single policy is encoded in the primitive, not that one number is wrong.

**Recommended canonical:** Add a density/touch mechanism to ui/button.tsx (size or data/CSS-variable driven: 44 below md, 36 or 32 from md) and DropdownMenuItem; delete the per-file constants. Choose one mobile height for Confirm/Cancel/Save groups.

**Verifier:** Per-file constants and breakpoint drift are plausible and the Button primitive (button.tsx:21-30) has no touch API. But the Inbox 36 px vs Portal 44 px split is a documented deliberate decision, so that part is intentional. Real issue: missing primitive plus sm:/md: drift and Settings staying at 36. Severity high overstated.

**Why it matters:** Profile, Security and Organization are sibling pages a manager visits in a row; Save moves left/right and swaps order between them, and Cancel is not a real cancel. This confirms and extends prior A5 and B2.

**Recommended canonical:** Extend Button with a pending prop (spinner via a single glyph with motion-reduce, aria-busy, disabled, optional pendingLabel) and make SubmitButton a thin wrapper that also forwards size/variant.

**Verifier:** SubmitButton has 32 non-story call sites (verified count), and several plain-Button variants with label swaps exist. Spinner without motion-reduce/aria-hidden in the shared component is a real small gap. Autosave and verbose-name cases are fine.

- Retry: outline sm with RotateCcw/RefreshCw icon (notifications), ghost xs with icon, outline sm without icon (Inbox), link default (Property look status bar). — [notification-list-notices.tsx:42](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-list-notices.tsx#L42), [notification-list-notices.tsx:62](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-list-notices.tsx#L62), [inbox-list-states.tsx:38](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-list-states.tsx#L38), [inbox-detail-sheet.tsx:140](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-detail-sheet.tsx#L140), [property-look-status-bar.tsx:42](../../../src/components/features/portal/property-look/property-look-status-bar.tsx#L42)
- Reload page / icon-only refresh / no control: notification loader says Reload page; Google performance uses an icon-only refresh with tooltip; Portal Results shows 'Please try again' with no button although ErrorState has one (confirms the Results part of prior C6). — [notification-popover-loader.tsx:36](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-popover-loader.tsx#L36), [google-performance-section.tsx:118-143](../../../src/components/features/property/google-performance-section.tsx#L118), [portal-analytics-tab.tsx:109-113](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-analytics/portal-analytics-tab.tsx#L109)

**Why it matters:** Error recovery is the highest-stress moment to meet an unfamiliar control; the same recovery should look and read the same, and at least one failure path offers no action.

**Could be intentional:** Inline link-style retry inside a sentence is a reasonable compact form; a bell popover may need Reload page.

**Recommended canonical:** ErrorState in layout/page-states.tsx (outline sm, 'Try again') plus one inline variant; adopt it in Notifications, Inbox and Portal panels.

**Verifier:** Not re-read line by line but the Try again vs Retry split and ErrorState bypass are credible; Portal Results without a retry button is the sharpest part. Bell popover Reload page is plausibly intentional.

- Dismiss the dialog: Cancel / Keep X / Close / Done: AlertDialogs use Cancel in nine places and bespoke Keep X (Keep Property, Keep archived, Keep connected, Keep invitation, Keep AI features on, Keep notifications, Keep them, Keep editing, Keep reviewing) in at least nine; completion uses Done (beta receipt, batch publish) or Close (version dialog, DialogFooter showCloseButton). — [portal-archive-dialog.tsx:70](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-archive-dialog.tsx#L70), [property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx:84](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx#L84), [property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx:134](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx#L134), [property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx:165](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx#L165), [notification-page.tsx:180](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-page.tsx#L180), [notification-default-controls.tsx:130](../../../src/components/features/settings/notification-default-controls.tsx#L130), [beta-feedback-receipt.tsx:42](../../../src/components/features/beta-feedback/beta-feedback-receipt.tsx#L42), [portal-version-dialog.tsx:152-155](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-version-dialog.tsx#L152)
- Remove / Delete / Archive: Remove member, Remove language, Remove from group, Delete link, Delete draft, Archive group/Portal/staff participation/Property, and Property 'Remove from workspace' which per its own comment is not a deletion but an archive-like state with a 30-day restore; invitation uses Cancel. — [property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx:30-52](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx#L30), [delete-link-dialog.tsx:30-39](../../../src/components/features/portal/link-tree/delete-link-dialog.tsx#L30), [portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx:46](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx#L46), [invitation-table.tsx:96-129](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/invitation-table.tsx#L96)

**Why it matters:** Recurring controls need a recognisable verb; differing capitalisation and the Remove/Delete/Archive mix make the same command look like different features and make consistency harder to grep.

**Could be intentional:** Property remove and Keep X wording are explicitly deliberate (comments explain the 30-day window and 'not delete'); domain-specific verbs for Portal and Property are fine. Capitalisation and Save/Update drift are not deliberate.

**Recommended canonical:** A short action-copy list: sentence case; Save <thing> only where a page holds several independent forms, otherwise Save changes; Cancel for dismissing, Keep X reserved for destructive dialogs; one Clear wording.

**Verifier:** Copy drift is real but the Keep X wording and Remove/Archive verbs are deliberate domain choices (acknowledged in the finding itself). Capitalisation and Save/Update drift are the actionable part; severity medium overstated.

**Could be intentional:** Portals has genuine secondary actions and a phone-specific bar; Members opens a dialog rather than navigating.

**Recommended canonical:** A shared PageHeader action recipe (default size, leading Plus at default sizing, sentence-case label, one touch policy).

**Verifier:** Header actions differ in casing and icon spelling; touch size drift overlaps ACT-03. Portals' multi-action header and phone bar are task-driven. Mostly casing/icon trivia.

### ACT-15 — Dialog and sheet close controls: bare 16 px X in the primitives vs Button-based closes with different labels

low · visible · divergent-implementations · adjusted

- Primitive X: DialogPrimitive.Close / SheetPrimitive.Close absolute top-4 right-4 rounded-xs opacity-70 with only the glyph as its hit area (no padding), focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-2, sr-only Close. — [dialog.tsx:63-69](../../../src/components/ui/dialog.tsx#L63), [sheet.tsx:72-75](../../../src/components/ui/sheet.tsx#L72)
- Button ghost icon closes: Notifications sheet (ghost icon-sm, 'Close notifications'), Inbox filter sheet (ghost icon size-9 -mr-2.5, 'Close'), Inbox detail (icon-sm max-md:size-9, 'Close detail'); the Inbox dialogs and sheets set showCloseButton={false} to draw their own. — [notification-sheet-header.tsx:14-23](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-sheet-header.tsx#L14), [inbox-filter-sheet.tsx:121-132](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-filter-sheet.tsx#L121), [inbox-detail-header.tsx:140-153](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-detail-header.tsx#L140), [feedback-handling-dialog.tsx:70](../../../src/components/inbox/feedback-handling-dialog.tsx#L70), [inbox-reopen-dialog.tsx:125](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-reopen-dialog.tsx#L125)
- Text Close: DialogFooter showCloseButton renders an outline Close; version dialog hand-rolls the same. — [dialog.tsx:100-105](../../../src/components/ui/dialog.tsx#L100), [portal-version-dialog.tsx:152-155](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-version-dialog.tsx#L152)

**Why it matters:** The default X is a tiny, low-opacity target that looks different from every Button-based close; different accessible names for the same job.

**Could be intentional:** Inbox suppresses the corner X deliberately for the mobile sheet header.

**Recommended canonical:** One DialogClose/SheetClose based on Button ghost icon-sm with touch sizing and a single 'Close' name pattern.

**Verifier:** Primitive X has a glyph-only hit area, and Inbox deliberately sets showCloseButton={false} for its mobile sheet header. Differing accessible names are minor. Not re-verified in detail.

### ACT-16 — Icon-only buttons rely on aria-label; tooltip only on one

low · behavioural · divergent-implementations · adjusted

- aria-label only (30 of 31 icon buttons): size is icon, icon-sm or icon-xs depending on region; no tooltip. — [notification-panel.tsx:183-189](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-panel.tsx#L183), [linktree-tile.tsx:171-176](../../../src/components/features/portal/link-tree/linktree-tile.tsx#L171)
- Tooltip: only the Google performance refresh button gets a Tooltip; sidebar nav uses its own. — [google-performance-section.tsx:125-141](../../../src/components/features/property/google-performance-section.tsx#L125)
- title attribute: ThemeToggle is the only Button that adds title; the Sidebar trigger uses an sr-only span instead of aria-label. — [theme-toggle.tsx:23-30](../../../src/components/layout/theme-toggle.tsx#L23), [sidebar.tsx:259-275](../../../src/components/ui/sidebar.tsx#L259)
- Missing aria-label match: the Inbox back button's aria-label sits after a long comment block; not a defect, but the pattern of documenting rather than reusing recurs. — [inbox-detail-header.tsx:96-103](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-detail-header.tsx#L96)

**Why it matters:** Icon buttons are well-labelled for screen readers, but sighted mouse users get a tooltip on one and nothing on the rest; sizes differ per region.

**Could be intentional:** Tooltips are not required on touch; aria-label is the accessible baseline.

**Recommended canonical:** aria-label as the minimum; adopt one IconButton wrapper (label prop, tooltip, touch size) if tooltips are wanted.

**Verifier:** Fourth variant ('missing aria-label match') is explicitly not a defect and should be dropped. aria-label baseline is fine; tooltip absence is a nice-to-have.

### ACT-17 — Portal Archive confirm is neutral primary while its menu item and sibling Archive group are destructive red

low · visible · behaviour-divergence · added-by-verifier

- Portal archive: menu item flagged destructive (third arg true) but AlertDialogAction has no variant, so confirm is default purple. — [portal-row-menu.ts:55](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-row-menu.ts#L55), [portal-archive-dialog.tsx:70-92](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-archive-dialog.tsx#L70)
- Group archive: same job, confirm hand-coloured destructive. — [portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx:40-42](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx#L40)

**Why it matters:** Same Archive command, different danger signal in sibling Portal dialogs, and the archive dialog also switches to Restore with the same primary styling.

**Could be intentional:** Archive is reversible for 30 days, so neutral may be deliberate, but then the red menu item contradicts it.

**Recommended canonical:** Decide one tone for archive; use AlertDialogAction variant prop in both.

### ACT-18 — Dashboard range control bypasses SegmentedControl that documents range as its use case

low · visible · primitive-bypassed · added-by-verifier

- Bypass: aria-pressed ghost/secondary Buttons (h-11) plus a Select below sm. — [dashboard-range-control.tsx:42-80](../../../src/components/features/dashboard/dashboard-range-control.tsx#L42)
- Primitive: header comment names 'range' as a use. — [segmented-control.tsx:1-10](../../../src/components/ui/segmented-control.tsx#L1)

**Why it matters:** Same range choice gets different semantics and look than the other segmented controls, and an unconditional h-11 on desktop.

**Could be intentional:** Mobile Select fallback may justify partial custom code.

**Recommended canonical:** ui/segmented-control.tsx with a responsive Select fallback.

### ACT-19 — Account menu Properties link inherits accent link colour while the other menu item does not

low · visible · primitive-misused · added-by-verifier

- Unpinned: asChild Link with no text-foreground! override; global anchor rule in styles.css:290-299 does not exclude dropdown-menu-item. — [header.tsx:46-48](../../../src/components/layout/header.tsx#L46), [styles.css:290-299](../../../src/styles.css#L290)

**Why it matters:** Other menus pin the ink with per-file ITEM constants; the top-level account menu renders a purple link next to a normal-ink Sign out item.

**Could be intentional:** none

**Recommended canonical:** Exclude [data-slot=dropdown-menu-item] in the global anchor rule so no call site needs the override.

- Security: bare div as a direct child of Card, left-aligned, Cancel then Update: not CardFooter; px-6 pb-2 div directly in Card. Cancel navigates to Profile; primary 'Update password'. — [security-settings-form.tsx:92](../../../src/components/features/identity/security-settings-form.tsx#L92), [security-settings-form.tsx:93](../../../src/components/features/identity/security-settings-form.tsx#L93), [security-settings-form.tsx:97](../../../src/components/features/identity/security-settings-form.tsx#L97)
- Organization: buttons outside the Card, Save first then Cancel, left-aligned, full width on phone: the only form where primary precedes Cancel and actions sit below the Card; Cancel navigates to Profile; 'Save changes' (lower-case c). — [organization-settings-form.tsx:91](../../../src/components/features/organization/organization-settings-form.tsx#L91), [organization-settings-form.tsx:92](../../../src/components/features/organization/organization-settings-form.tsx#L92), [organization-settings-form.tsx:99](../../../src/components/features/organization/organization-settings-form.tsx#L99)
- Button inside the card body, left-aligned, no footer: Private feedback target (Save Property target) and Goal create put the primary inside CardContent. Timezone/format puts a w-fit Save inside the field grid and is the only settings form that disables Save until something changed. — [private-feedback-target-card.tsx:132](../../../src/components/features/property/private-feedback-target-card.tsx#L132), [goals/new.tsx:267](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/new.tsx#L267), [goals/new.tsx:269](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/properties/$propertyId/goals/new.tsx#L269), [notification-formatting-form.tsx:166](../../../src/components/features/settings/notification-formatting-form.tsx#L166), [notification-formatting-form.tsx:173](../../../src/components/features/settings/notification-formatting-form.tsx#L173)
- Per-sub-form Save inside bordered mini panels: Organization response targets: each target is its own rounded-lg border form with 'Save target' inside a Card with no footer. Quiet hours has an outline 'Save quiet hours' inside the editor row. Responsible managers has a right-aligned Save in its own panel with 'Saving…' text rather than the spinner. — [response-target-settings-card.tsx:151](../../../src/components/features/organization/response-target-settings-card.tsx#L151), [response-target-settings-card.tsx:178](../../../src/components/features/organization/response-target-settings-card.tsx#L178), [quiet-hours-editor.tsx:55](../../../src/components/features/settings/quiet-hours-editor.tsx#L55), [quiet-hours-editor.tsx:60](../../../src/components/features/settings/quiet-hours-editor.tsx#L60), [responsible-managers-panel.tsx:150](../../../src/components/features/responsible-managers/responsible-managers-panel.tsx#L150), [responsible-managers-panel.tsx:153](../../../src/components/features/responsible-managers/responsible-managers-panel.tsx#L153)
- AI card footer: destructive-ish action first, primary last, 44px, stacked on phone: CardFooter flex-col sm:flex-row sm:justify-end with Turn off (outline) before Save feature access (primary), both min-h-11 w-full on phone and a hand-placed Loader2 instead of SubmitButton's spinner. — [merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx:41](../../../src/components/features/settings/merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx#L41), [merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx:47](../../../src/components/features/settings/merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx#L47), [merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx:76](../../../src/components/features/settings/merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx#L76)
- No shared dirty/discard contract: Save is dirty-gated in four places only (timezone/format, responsible managers, AI Save, goal assignments) and always enabled elsewhere. No explicit-save settings form has a leave guard or dirty indicator; the only useBlocker is the Portal editor prompt. Cancel never resets (B2). — [responsible-managers-panel.tsx:152](../../../src/components/features/responsible-managers/responsible-managers-panel.tsx#L152), [merchant-ai-property-authorization.tsx:149](../../../src/components/features/settings/merchant-ai-property-authorization.tsx#L149), [goal-program-assignments-dialog.tsx:112](../../../src/components/goals/goal-program-assignments-dialog.tsx#L112), [portal-unsaved-changes-prompt.tsx:42](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-detail/portal-unsaved-changes-prompt.tsx#L42)

**Why it matters:** Moving Profile -> Security -> Organization (three adjacent sidebar entries) puts the primary action at three positions with three alignments, two orders and two capitalisations of the same verb. Users learn nothing transferable and a shared fix (reset, dirty state, pending) cannot land once.

**Could be intentional:** Immediate/read-only/confirmed-command sections legitimately have no footer, and AI enable/revoke is a command pair rather than a form save. None of the explicit-save forms has a domain reason to differ in placement or order.

**Recommended canonical:** Property business profile pattern (Card + CardHeader + CardFooter justify-end, primary only, no Cancel) promoted to a shared explicit-save section recipe (header, body, banner slot, FormActions footer with optional real Reset). Ban bare divs and out-of-card buttons for explicit-save sections.

**Verifier:** Spot-checked Profile (Cancel+Save Changes, in CardContent, right), Security (bare div in Card, left, Update password), Org (outside Card, Save then Cancel, Save changes). Accurate. Severity could be medium but same function clearly diverges.

- Plain frame and unboxed sections: Portal editor frame h2 text-lg tracking-tight no border; Members h2 text-base semibold sections with tables and h3 'Pending Invitations'. — [portal-editor-section-frame.tsx:30](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-editor-section-frame.tsx#L30), [members.tsx:219](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/settings/members.tsx#L219), [invitation-table.tsx:57](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/invitation-table.tsx#L57)
- Heading hierarchy breaks (A6): Organization has two h1s; Notifications puts h3 and div role=heading aria-level=3 under non-heading Card titles; Profile repeats 'Profile' as page and card title; Portal editor nests h3 'Responsible managers' under h2 'Responsible'. — [organization-settings-page.tsx:113](../../../src/components/features/organization/organization-settings-page.tsx#L113), [page-header.tsx:68](../../../src/components/layout/page-header.tsx#L68), [notification-quiet-hours-card.tsx:114](../../../src/components/features/settings/notification-quiet-hours-card.tsx#L114), [notifications-category-row.tsx:219](../../../src/components/features/settings/notifications-category-row.tsx#L219), [profile-settings-form.tsx:93](../../../src/components/features/identity/profile-settings-form.tsx#L93), [portal-editor-section-frame.tsx:30](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-editor-section-frame.tsx#L30), [responsible-managers-panel.tsx:93](../../../src/components/features/responsible-managers/responsible-managers-panel.tsx#L93)

**Why it matters:** Sections that are the same kind of thing have different radius, padding, border rules and title weights, and assistive tech sees headings skipping levels or duplicating h1s.

**Could be intentional:** Property look's label-left rows and the Portal editor's full-bleed frame belong to a different layout family. Inside the management Card family the extra bordered panels and heading variance are accidental.

**Recommended canonical:** ui/Card extended into a SettingsSection recipe (semantic heading level prop, description, notice slot, footer), used for Property settings and Portal editor panels.

**Verifier:** Org page really has an h1 in the body at organization-settings-page.tsx:113 plus PageHeader h1 (real a11y issue). The rest mixes Portal editor / Property look / lifecycle, which are different layout families the owner accepts. Narrow to management-Card family plus heading hierarchy.

## Overlays, surfaces and feedback (SURF)

### Inventory

| Pattern                                          | Canonical                                                                       | Implementations                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Modal dialog (form, detail, picker)              | ui/dialog.tsx                                                                   | Beta feedback launcher (xl, max-h 100vh, X, left-note footer with Kbd); Leave organization (default lg, no max-h, destructive primary only, no failure surface); Invite member (default lg, bare submit); Add staff / Portal responsibilities (Cancel-disabled-only); Portal new (640px, dvh bound); Group create/rename/add (shared CONTENT const, stale-error guard); Upload dialog shell + 3 callers (dvh bound, full dismissal block, ghost Cancel); Property look batch publish; Portal version dialog (X plus Close); Goal program dialogs (2xl, max-h 90vh, no Cancel, native select); Reply template editor (4xl, X forced to 44px); Inbox feedback-handling / reopen / shortcuts (no X, 36px actions) |
| Destructive/consequential confirmation           | property-lifecycle-confirmation-dialog.tsx (partial shell over ui/alert-dialog) | ConfirmationDialog shell (no variant prop; 4 callers); Remove member / Cancel invitation (text-white); Delete link / Leave without saving / Archive group / Stop codes (text-destructive-foreground); Disable public page / AI revoke / Replace code (variant=destructive); Archive portal / Archive staff / Dismiss all / Reset defaults (primary confirm); Publish/Update Google reply (preventDefault guard; Reject inline); unconfirmed disconnect (google-connection-settings-row.tsx)                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Sheet / drawer                                   | ui/sheet.tsx                                                                    | Inbox detail sheet; Inbox filter sheet (bottom, 85dvh); Notification sheet (notification-panel.tsx); Sidebar mobile drawer (ui/sidebar.tsx)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

### SURF-01 — Destructive confirm buttons are styled four ways; four use a token pair that is red-on-red in light mode

high · visible · primitive-bypassed · confirmed

- Primitive variant: AlertDialogAction forwards Button's `destructive` variant (adds dark:bg-destructive/60). Only 3 call sites use it. Surfaces: Portal overview, Property AI settings, Portal share. — [alert-dialog.tsx:131-143](../../../src/components/ui/alert-dialog.tsx#L131), [button.tsx:13-14](../../../src/components/ui/button.tsx#L13), [portal-disable-dialog.tsx:51-52](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-disable-dialog.tsx#L51), [merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx:66](../../../src/components/features/settings/merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx#L66), [portal-replace-code-form.tsx:118](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-share/portal-replace-code-form.tsx#L118)
- Hand-rolled bg-destructive text-white: class string copied onto AlertDialogAction. Skips the primitive's dark-mode step, so dark red is brighter than the variant. Surfaces: Settings > Members. — [invitation-table.tsx:125](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/invitation-table.tsx#L125), [remove-member-dialog.tsx:51](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/remove-member-dialog.tsx#L51)
- Hand-rolled bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground: four buttons pair bg-destructive with text-destructive-foreground. In light mode --destructive-foreground is oklch(0.58 0.22 25), the same value as --destructive, so the label is the fill colour (source-derived, not rendered). Dark mode (near-white) is fine. The token is a red-text token, not an on-fill token. Surfaces: Portal editor, Portal groups, Portal share, Property look. — [styles.css:50](../../../src/styles.css#L50), [styles.css:131](../../../src/styles.css#L131), [styles.css:207](../../../src/styles.css#L207), [delete-link-dialog.tsx:36](../../../src/components/features/portal/link-tree/delete-link-dialog.tsx#L36), [portal-unsaved-changes-prompt.tsx:65](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-detail/portal-unsaved-changes-prompt.tsx#L65), [portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx:42](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx#L42), [portal-revoke-links-form.tsx:67](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-share/portal-revoke-links-form.tsx#L67)
- Primary-coloured confirm for an equivalent consequential action: the shared ConfirmationDialog shell has no variant prop, so Property 'Remove from workspace' opens from a solid red trigger but confirms in purple. Archive Portal and Archive staff participation also confirm in primary, while Archive group confirms in red. — [property-lifecycle-confirmation-dialog.tsx:52](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-confirmation-dialog.tsx#L52), [property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx:30](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx#L30), [property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx:41](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx#L41), [portal-archive-dialog.tsx:71](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-archive-dialog.tsx#L71), [staff-participation-row.tsx:111](../../../src/components/features/staff/staff-participation-row.tsx#L111), [portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx:41-42](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx#L41), [notification-page.tsx:181](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-page.tsx#L181)
- Trigger treatment for destructive intent: triggers are outline + text-destructive (Remove member, Cancel invitation), ghost + hover:text-destructive (staff archive), solid destructive (Property remove), or plain outline (AI revoke, Archive Property). — [remove-member-dialog.tsx:30-34](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/remove-member-dialog.tsx#L30), [invitation-table.tsx:98-102](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/invitation-table.tsx#L98), [staff-participation-row.tsx:90-94](../../../src/components/features/staff/staff-participation-row.tsx#L90), [merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx:46-48](../../../src/components/features/settings/merchant-ai-settings-actions.tsx#L46)

**Why it matters:** The colour of the confirm button is the user's last cue that an action is destructive. Today an equivalent action turns red in one dialog and purple in the next, and four buttons are probably unreadable in light mode. Adding a variant to the shared shell is a single-file fix.

**Could be intentional:** Reversible archive/restore may deliberately avoid red, but Archive group is red and Archive portal is not, and the red-on-red token pair cannot be deliberate.

**Recommended canonical:** AlertDialogAction variant="destructive" (ui/alert-dialog.tsx). Add a `tone`/`variant` prop to ConfirmationDialog and route the other AlertDialogs through it. Fix or retire --destructive-foreground.

**Verifier:** Checked: styles.css:50 and :131 both oklch(0.58 0.22 25), so the 4 bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground buttons are red-on-red in light mode (dark :207 is fine). Two bg-destructive text-white confirms exist (remove-member-dialog:51, invitation-table:125). Property remove trigger is variant=destructive while the shared shell action has no variant (property-lifecycle-confirmation-dialog.tsx:52).

### SURF-02 — Same confirmation job uses AlertDialog, Dialog, inline panel, or nothing (20 AlertDialog files outside ui/)

high · behavioural · behaviour-divergence · adjusted

- AlertDialog, hand-built per site (the majority): 18 files use ui/alert-dialog, mostly each with its own header/footer; only the 4 Property lifecycle dialogs share a shell. — [property-lifecycle-confirmation-dialog.tsx:16-57](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-confirmation-dialog.tsx#L16), [remove-member-dialog.tsx:38-56](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/remove-member-dialog.tsx#L38), [delete-link-dialog.tsx:25-42](../../../src/components/features/portal/link-tree/delete-link-dialog.tsx#L25)
- Dialog used as a destructive confirm: Leave organization is a plain Dialog with a destructive 'Transfer and leave' button, an X as the only dismissal, and no Cancel. — [leave-organization-dialog.tsx:152-244](../../../src/components/features/people/leave-organization-dialog.tsx#L152)
- AlertDialog hosting a form: Replace code, Stop all codes and Archive Property put radios, number inputs or a textarea inside AlertDialog, while other forms-with-a-destructive-outcome use Dialog. — [portal-replace-code-form.tsx:53-128](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-share/portal-replace-code-form.tsx#L53), [portal-revoke-links-form.tsx:61-75](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-share/portal-revoke-links-form.tsx#L61), [property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx:90-104](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx#L90)
- Inline expand instead of overlay: Reject reply opens an inline reason field with a red 'Confirm Reject'. Make-version-live confirms inline in the ledger row on desktop and inside a Dialog when started from the version rail, for the same action. — [reply-message-actions.tsx:290-340](../../../src/components/inbox/reply-message-actions.tsx#L290), [portal-history-view.tsx:49-70](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-history-view.tsx#L49), [portal-history-view.tsx:151-175](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-history-view.tsx#L151), [portal-version-dialog.tsx:99-111](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-version-dialog.tsx#L99)
- No confirmation: disconnecting an organization Google account is a single click that revokes the connection and is reported only by an error toast. Disconnecting a Property from Google is routed to the danger zone and confirmed. — [google-connection-settings-row.tsx:90-96](../../../src/components/features/settings/google-connection-settings-row.tsx#L90), [integrations-settings-page.tsx:52-57](../../../src/components/features/settings/integrations-settings-page.tsx#L52), [property-google-section.tsx:115-125](../../../src/components/features/property/settings/property-google-section.tsx#L115), [property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx:145-170](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx#L145)
- Cancel wording: 'Cancel' on 9 AlertDialogs; 'Keep …' on 11 others, each with its own noun ('Keep invitation', 'Keep editing', 'Keep them', 'Keep Property active', 'Keep reviewing'). — [invitation-table.tsx:117](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/invitation-table.tsx#L117), [notification-page.tsx:180](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-page.tsx#L180), [notification-default-controls.tsx:130](../../../src/components/features/settings/notification-default-controls.tsx#L130), [property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx:40](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-dialogs.tsx#L40), [reply-message-actions.tsx:272](../../../src/components/inbox/reply-message-actions.tsx#L272)

**Why it matters:** Users cannot predict whether an action asks first. A destructive org-level Google disconnect is one click while removing a member or a link is gated. The same restore confirmation changes host with the entry point.

**Could be intentional:** Inline confirm for reject/restore keeps context beside the thing acted on, and Keep-wording is sometimes clearer than Cancel. The ungated Google disconnect has no recorded rationale.

**Recommended canonical:** One ConfirmationDialog shell (extend property-lifecycle-confirmation-dialog.tsx: tone, optional body slot, standard pending/close contract) over ui/alert-dialog; a stated rule for when an inline confirm is allowed.

**Verifier:** Substance holds: org Google Disconnect is a single click with no gate (google-connection-settings-row.tsx:90-96); the leave dialog is a plain Dialog. The count is 20 files outside ui/, not 18. The 'Cancel on 9 AlertDialogs' figure holds.

### SURF-03 — Pending-state dismissal: three policies, and AlertDialog pending UI cannot show

high · behavioural · behaviour-divergence · confirmed

- Whole dialog blocked while committing: Escape, overlay and X are all blocked. — [property-look-batch-dialog.tsx:65-68](../../../src/components/features/portal/property-look/property-look-batch-dialog.tsx#L65), [inbox-reopen-dialog.tsx:108-111](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-reopen-dialog.tsx#L108), [upload-dialog-shell.tsx:34-38](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-media/upload-dialog-shell.tsx#L34)
- Only the Cancel button disabled: Escape, overlay and X still close the dialog mid-request. — [portal-new-form.tsx:109](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-new/portal-new-form.tsx#L109), [portal-new-dialog.tsx:29](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-new/portal-new-dialog.tsx#L29), [feedback-handling-form.tsx:131-139](../../../src/components/inbox/feedback-handling-form.tsx#L131), [feedback-handling-dialog.tsx:35](../../../src/components/inbox/feedback-handling-dialog.tsx#L35), [portal-responsibilities-modal.tsx:80](../../../src/components/features/staff/portal-responsibilities-modal.tsx#L80), [portal-responsibilities-modal.tsx:127-133](../../../src/components/features/staff/portal-responsibilities-modal.tsx#L127), [beta-feedback-form.tsx:219](../../../src/components/features/beta-feedback/beta-feedback-form.tsx#L219)
- No guard at all: Cancel stays enabled, no onOpenChange guard. — [reply-template-editor.tsx:139](../../../src/components/features/property/reply-template-editor.tsx#L139), [reply-template-editor.tsx:253](../../../src/components/features/property/reply-template-editor.tsx#L253), [portal-group-create-form.tsx:86](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-create-form.tsx#L86), [goal-program-form-dialog.tsx:35](../../../src/components/goals/goal-program-form-dialog.tsx#L35), [members.tsx:192](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/settings/members.tsx#L192), [staff-tab.tsx:94](../../../src/components/features/property/people/staff-tab.tsx#L94)
- AlertDialog pending state is unreachable: AlertDialogAction renders Radix Dialog.Close, so activating it closes the dialog. The `isPending ? 'Removing…'`, 'Archiving…', 'Disconnecting…' labels and `disabled={isPending}` on the confirm action can only flash during the close animation. None of these actions call preventDefault; only the reply publish/update confirms do, and only to block. Replace-code and Stop-codes close the dialog on submit start. Failures then surface outside the dialog (toast/banner/nothing). — [index.mjs:85](../../../node_modules/@radix-ui/react-alert-dialog/dist/index.mjs#L85), [remove-member-dialog.tsx:48-54](../../../src/components/features/identity/member-directory/remove-member-dialog.tsx#L48), [portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx:41-47](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-archive-dialog.tsx#L41), [portal-archive-dialog.tsx:71-90](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-archive-dialog.tsx#L71), [staff-participation-row.tsx:111-125](../../../src/components/features/staff/staff-participation-row.tsx#L111), [property-lifecycle-confirmation-dialog.tsx:52-54](../../../src/components/features/property/property-lifecycle-confirmation-dialog.tsx#L52), [portal-replace-code-form.tsx:46](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-share/portal-replace-code-form.tsx#L46), [portal-stop-codes-dialog.tsx:36](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-share/portal-stop-codes-dialog.tsx#L36), [reply-message-actions.tsx:249](../../../src/components/inbox/reply-message-actions.tsx#L249)
- Leave organization has no failure surface: 'Transfer and leave' calls the mutation with `void` and no catch; leaveMutation has no errorMessage and the dialog renders no banner, so a refusal produces neither a toast nor an inline message (source-derived). — [leave-organization-dialog.tsx:224-243](../../../src/components/features/people/leave-organization-dialog.tsx#L224), [members.tsx:160-165](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/settings/members.tsx#L160)

**Why it matters:** A user can close a half-committed dialog by Escape in one place and not in another, and the confirm buttons' progress labels are dead code. The highest-stakes dialog (leave organization) can fail silently.

**Could be intentional:** Fast, idempotent actions may tolerate dismissal. Nothing in the code states that choice; the prior B7 and the draft pattern-spec both call for one policy.

**Recommended canonical:** Enforce the policy once in a shared mutation-dialog wrapper (the upload shell and batch dialog already do). For AlertDialog confirms, preventDefault on the action and close on settle, or move committing confirms to a Dialog with the guard.

**Verifier:** Radix AlertDialogAction renders DialogPrimitive.Close (react-alert-dialog 1.1.23 index.mjs:85), so the pending label and disabled state on the confirm are dead unless preventDefault is called. The leave dialog's 'Transfer and leave' uses void leaveOrganization(...) with no catch. leaveMutation (members.tsx:162-167) has successMessage, navigateTo and invalidateKeys but no errorMessage, and the dialog has no error surface.

medium · visible · divergent-implementations · adjusted

- Both channels for one failure: Notification timezone/format shows toast.error and the banner for the same error. - Same upload failure, two channels, different message policy: ImageUploadField (avatar, org logo) toasts file-type, size and upload errors, passing the raw err.message; the property-look and link-tree upload dialogs print an inline alert. useActionMutation deliberately hides 5xx text, the upload hook does not. — [use-file-upload.ts:31](../../../src/components/forms/image-upload-field/use-file-upload.ts#L31), [use-file-upload.ts:35](../../../src/components/forms/image-upload-field/use-file-upload.ts#L35), [use-file-upload.ts:50-61](../../../src/components/forms/image-upload-field/use-file-upload.ts#L50), [use-action-mutation.ts:46-51](../../../src/components/hooks/use-action-mutation.ts#L46), [property-look-photo-dialog.tsx:209](../../../src/components/features/portal/property-look/property-look-photo-dialog.tsx#L209), [linktree-photo-dialog.tsx:169](../../../src/components/features/portal/link-tree/linktree-photo-dialog.tsx#L169)
- Success shown as toast, inline note, Alert, or in-dialog receipt: most saves toast. Goal revision prints a muted `<p role=status>`; Setup shows a default Alert with a check icon; beta feedback replaces the dialog body with a receipt; each notification toggle toasts while Portal editor autosave uses a status chip. — [goal-program-revision-dialog.tsx:159-163](../../../src/components/goals/goal-program-revision-dialog.tsx#L159), [setup-results.tsx:54-62](../../../src/components/features/property-setup/setup-results.tsx#L54), [beta-feedback-receipt.tsx:20](../../../src/components/features/beta-feedback/beta-feedback-receipt.tsx#L20), [use-notification-preference-saves.ts:108](../../../src/components/features/settings/use-notification-preference-saves.ts#L108), [portal-draft-save-status.tsx:27-46](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-editor/portal-draft-save-status.tsx#L27)
- Copy conventions: failure toasts start 'Failed to …' (2), 'Could not …' (3) or 'Couldn't …' (many), with and without a final period; success says '… updated successfully' (profile, avatar, password) but 'Role updated', 'Property profile saved' elsewhere. — [integrations-settings-page.tsx:56](../../../src/components/features/settings/integrations-settings-page.tsx#L56), [organization-settings-page.tsx:71](../../../src/components/features/organization/organization-settings-page.tsx#L71), [notification-quiet-hours-card.tsx:92](../../../src/components/features/settings/notification-quiet-hours-card.tsx#L92), [notification-formatting-form.tsx:103](../../../src/components/features/settings/notification-formatting-form.tsx#L103), [notification-mutations.ts:105](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-mutations.ts#L105), [profile.tsx:46](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/settings/profile.tsx#L46), [security.tsx:14](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/settings/security.tsx#L14), [members.tsx:127](../../../src/routes/_authenticated/settings/members.tsx#L127)

**Why it matters:** Users see the same kind of failure as a top-right toast in one form, a red banner in the next, a small red sentence in a third, and not at all in the leave dialog. Some failures are announced twice, and some surface raw server text.

**Could be intentional:** Row/table actions without an inline surface reasonably toast, and ephemeral toggles may reasonably toast. The overlap and raw-message cases are not deliberate.

**Recommended canonical:** One ownership rule per operation type (form submit = FormErrorBanner, row/immediate action = toast via useActionMutation errorMessage, never both) and one copy rule; route upload errors through actionErrorMessage.

**Verifier:** Real but a breadth finding made of many small variants; high is overstated. FormErrorBanner is in 51 files (claimed 46). The leave-dialog silent failure is already counted in SURF-03. The copy-convention sub-variant is low severity.

### SURF-05 — Alert and Badge have no semantic tones, so warning/success/info callouts are rebuilt by hand

medium · visible · missing-shared-primitive · confirmed

- Alert primitive: two variants: ui/alert has `default` and `destructive`; both keep bg-card and only recolour text/icon. medium · visible · divergent-implementations · adjusted

- Width: default sm:max-w-lg (6 dialogs), sm:max-w-sm, md, xl (x3), arbitrary 640px, 2xl, 2xl->4xl, 4xl and min(48rem,…); chosen per dialog by arbitrary values and a different spelling each time. — [dialog.tsx:54](../../../src/components/ui/dialog.tsx#L54), [inbox-shortcuts-dialog.tsx:22](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-shortcuts-dialog.tsx#L22), [portal-group-dialogs.tsx:24](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-dialogs.tsx#L24), [portal-group-dialogs.tsx:71](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-group/portal-group-dialogs.tsx#L71), [portal-new-dialog.tsx:30](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-new/portal-new-dialog.tsx#L30), [goal-program-form-dialog.tsx:39](../../../src/components/goals/goal-program-form-dialog.tsx#L39), [property-look-photo-dialog.tsx:53](../../../src/components/features/portal/property-look/property-look-photo-dialog.tsx#L53), [portal-version-dialog.tsx:104](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-version-dialog.tsx#L104), [reply-template-editor.tsx:141](../../../src/components/features/property/reply-template-editor.tsx#L141)
- Height bound: of 17 DialogContent sites, 8 bound height (5 use 100dvh-2rem, 2 use 100vh-2rem, 1 uses 90vh) and 9 do not. Leave organization renders one Select per responsibility with no bound, so a long list pushes the fixed, centred dialog past the viewport and the footer can become unreachable (source-derived). — [upload-dialog-shell.tsx:40-42](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-media/upload-dialog-shell.tsx#L40), [beta-feedback-launcher.tsx:103-104](../../../src/components/features/beta-feedback/beta-feedback-launcher.tsx#L103), [goal-program-form-dialog.tsx:39](../../../src/components/goals/goal-program-form-dialog.tsx#L39), [leave-organization-dialog.tsx:152](../../../src/components/features/people/leave-organization-dialog.tsx#L152), [leave-organization-dialog.tsx:183-222](../../../src/components/features/people/leave-organization-dialog.tsx#L183), [staff-tab.tsx:101](../../../src/components/features/property/people/staff-tab.tsx#L101), [inbox-reopen-dialog.tsx:125](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-reopen-dialog.tsx#L125)
- Close affordance: primitive X (16px) by default; hidden in the two Inbox dialogs with the Cancel button as the exit; enlarged to 44px by a descendant selector in the reply template editor; Version dialog shows both an X and a footer Close button. The three sheets also drop the primitive X and rebuild a header. — [dialog.tsx:62-72](../../../src/components/ui/dialog.tsx#L62), [feedback-handling-dialog.tsx:70](../../../src/components/inbox/feedback-handling-dialog.tsx#L70), [inbox-reopen-dialog.tsx:125](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-reopen-dialog.tsx#L125), [reply-template-editor.tsx:141](../../../src/components/features/property/reply-template-editor.tsx#L141), [portal-version-dialog.tsx:153-155](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-version-dialog.tsx#L153)
- Phone action height: Inbox dialogs explicitly keep the 36px default (WCAG 2.5.8 reasoning in comments); the reply template editor forces min-h-11; the draft pattern-spec says dialog actions should be 44px. — [inbox-reopen-dialog.tsx:207-222](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-reopen-dialog.tsx#L207), [feedback-handling-dialog.tsx:36-62](../../../src/components/inbox/feedback-handling-dialog.tsx#L36), [reply-template-editor.tsx:253-258](../../../src/components/features/property/reply-template-editor.tsx#L253), the pattern-spec draft note

**Why it matters:** Dialogs jump between widths and some can clip on short screens. The close control is a different size or missing depending on the feature, and the next dialog a user opens may or may not have an X.

**Could be intentional:** Content-driven widths (a 4xl editor with preview) are plausible; the vh/dvh mix, missing height bound and per-feature X handling are not.

**Recommended canonical:** Add `size` (sm/md/lg/xl) and a built-in max-h/overflow-y-auto using dvh to ui/dialog DialogContent, as AlertDialogContent already does with `size`; keep one documented close rule.

**Verifier:** Plausible and consistent with the code opened (leave dialog has no height bound; the inbox dialogs hide the X). The 17 sites and width spellings were not re-counted; the original grep for DialogContent returned nothing under the include flag used. The WCAG-driven 36px inbox buttons are deliberate and should be dropped from the evidence.

### SURF-09 — Toaster ignores the app's theme and uses Sonner's own palette for typed toasts

medium · visible · visual-token-drift · confirmed

- Rich colours and theme: Toaster is mounted with richColors and no `theme` prop; the wrapper maps only --normal-bg/text/border to popover tokens. Sonner 2.0.8 defaults theme to 'light' and its rich-colour palettes are hsl literals (e.g. --success-bg hsl(143 85% 96%)), so success/error/warning/info toasts will not follow the .dark class or --success/--destructive/--warn tokens (source-derived, not rendered). — [__root.tsx:120](../../../src/routes/__root.tsx#L120), [sonner.tsx:12-31](../../../src/components/ui/sonner.tsx#L12), [styles.css](../../../node_modules/sonner/dist/styles.css)
- Mixed stack: plain toast() (notification dismiss/undo/urgent arrivals) uses the themed normal colours while typed toasts use the rich palette, so one stack can contain two visual families. Toast icons come from a third icon set. — [notification-mutations.ts:207-258](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-mutations.ts#L207), [notification-urgent-arrivals.ts:83](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-urgent-arrivals.ts#L83), [sonner.tsx:15-21](../../../src/components/ui/sonner.tsx#L15)

**Why it matters:** Feedback colours should mean the same thing everywhere; a pastel light success toast on a dark UI is a likely dark-mode gap.

**Could be intentional:** richColors may have been chosen for fast severity cues; ignoring the theme and tokens almost certainly was not.

**Recommended canonical:** ui/sonner.tsx driven by the app's theme mode and token-based --success-bg/--error-bg/--warning-bg variables.

**Verifier:** __root.tsx:120 mounts Toaster with richColors and no theme. sonner.tsx maps only normal-* vars. sonner defaults theme='light' (index.mjs:983) and the rich palettes are hsl literals (light and dark sets keyed on data-sonner-theme). So typed toasts will not follow the app's .dark class or the tokens.

medium · visible · behaviour-divergence · confirmed

- Alert + outline button: route boundary renders destructive Alert + 'Try again' and, for 404, a non-destructive Alert; Setup results use an Alert with 'Try again' inside. — [router.tsx:57-70](../../../src/router.tsx#L57), [router.tsx:77-83](../../../src/router.tsx#L77), [setup-results.tsx:64-90](../../../src/components/features/property-setup/setup-results.tsx#L64)
- EmptyState with small retry: ErrorState helper: icon, title, sm outline retry. — [page-states.tsx:31-42](../../../src/components/layout/page-states.tsx#L31)
- Bare flex row: role=alert sentence with an outline sm retry, and the state-specific verb 'Check again'. — [timezone-and-format-card.tsx:81-93](../../../src/components/features/settings/timezone-and-format-card.tsx#L81), [email-availability-notice.tsx:24-35](../../../src/components/features/settings/email-availability-notice.tsx#L24)
- Sentence with no retry: Results tab says 'try again' but offers no action; the version rail has none. — [portal-analytics-tab.tsx:109-114](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-analytics/portal-analytics-tab.tsx#L109), [portal-versions-rail.tsx:146-149](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-versions-rail.tsx#L146)
- Sentence plus link-style or outline retry in the same History pane: Ledger uses outline 'Try again'; selection detail uses link buttons 'Try again'/'Cancel'; the dialog uses a link button. — [portal-history-ledger.tsx:73-85](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-history-ledger.tsx#L73), [portal-history-view.tsx:90-99](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-history-view.tsx#L90), [portal-version-dialog.tsx:141-149](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-version-dialog.tsx#L141)
- Retry wording: 'Try again' in about 20 places, 'Retry' in inbox and property-look status, 'Retry save', 'Retry this property', 'Check again'. — [inbox-detail-sheet.tsx:146](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-detail-sheet.tsx#L146), [inbox-list-states.tsx:39](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-list-states.tsx#L39), [property-look-status-bar.tsx:48](../../../src/components/features/portal/property-look/property-look-status-bar.tsx#L48), [google-import-progress-items.tsx:73](../../../src/components/features/integration/google-import-manager/google-import-progress-items.tsx#L73)

**Why it matters:** A failed region sometimes tells the user what to do and offers the action, sometimes only the first, sometimes neither.

**Could be intentional:** Inbox's 'Retry' is the pane's own vocabulary and a compact link retry may suit a dense rail. A missing retry is not.

**Recommended canonical:** One RegionError (Alert-based, destructive tone) with title, sentence, and 'Try again' action, with a size for rails and dialogs; ErrorState/EmptyState (error tone) for page-level.

**Verifier:** Spot-checked the retry variants across router, page-states, setup-results and the History pane. The inconsistency in presentation and wording is real; dense rail vs page is a partial justification.

### SURF-11 — Colour token drift: raw palette, two red-text tokens, literal colours

low · visible · visual-token-drift · adjusted

- Raw Tailwind palette where tokens exist: Google import status uses emerald-600 and amber-600 next to token text-destructive; a trend helper (no importers found) uses emerald-500/red-500; portal stars use amber-500 where other stars use the --rating token. — [google-import-progress-items.tsx:38-43](../../../src/components/features/integration/google-import-manager/google-import-progress-items.tsx#L38), [property-dashboard-helpers.tsx:12-13](../../../src/components/features/property/property-dashboard-helpers.tsx#L12), [portal-results-strip.tsx:15](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-analytics/portal-results-strip.tsx#L15), [portal-overview-measure-cells.tsx:39](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-measure-cells.tsx#L39), [property-list-cells.tsx:85](../../../src/components/features/property/property-list-cells.tsx#L85), [star-rating.tsx:74](../../../src/components/ui/star-rating.tsx#L74)
- text-destructive vs text-negative for the same error line: field errors and most inline errors use text-destructive (53 uses; ui/field.tsx); the property-look dialogs and batch publish use text-negative (23 uses), as do other round-4 surfaces. Two red-text tokens for one meaning. — [field.tsx:117](../../../src/components/ui/field.tsx#L117), [property-look-batch-dialog.tsx:88](../../../src/components/features/portal/property-look/property-look-batch-dialog.tsx#L88), [property-look-media-actions.tsx:34](../../../src/components/features/portal/property-look/property-look-media-actions.tsx#L34), [portal-version-dialog.tsx:143](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-version-dialog.tsx#L143), [portal-restore-confirmation.tsx:126](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-history/portal-restore-confirmation.tsx#L126)
- Literal colours: Header logo dot uses a hard-coded oklch gradient (DESIGN.md says no gradients); body selection colour is an oklch literal; logo previews use neutral-900/400 (probably intended to simulate a dark page); ui/sidebar uses hsl(var(--sidebar-border)) while tokens are oklch (variant has no callers). — [header.tsx:21](../../../src/components/layout/header.tsx#L21), [__root.tsx:102](../../../src/routes/__root.tsx#L102), [property-look-logo-dialog.tsx:64](../../../src/components/features/portal/property-look/property-look-logo-dialog.tsx#L64), [sidebar.tsx:472](../../../src/components/ui/sidebar.tsx#L472), [DESIGN.md:109](../../../DESIGN.md#L109)
- Arbitrary type sizes: text-[13px] (18 uses), text-[11px], text-[10px], text-[9px], mostly in the Inbox and primitives; the count-badge recipe is duplicated. — [fact.tsx:39](../../../src/components/ui/fact.tsx#L39), [segmented-control.tsx:55](../../../src/components/ui/segmented-control.tsx#L55), [notification-panel.tsx:195](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-panel.tsx#L195), [app-top-bar.tsx:99](../../../src/components/layout/app-top-bar.tsx#L99)

**Why it matters:** Status colours change meaning depending on which token the author reached for, and some will not follow the theme or contrast fixes made to the tokens.

**Could be intentional:** Dark logo previews and the inbox's dense type scale look deliberate. The palette and token splits do not.

**Recommended canonical:** text-destructive for field/inline errors (text-negative only for directional numerics), text-positive/text-warn/text-rating for status, and a lint rule against raw palette classes.

**Verifier:** Counts are off: text-negative is 25 uses (not 23), text-destructive 72 (not 53). Real but low-impact drift; the dark logo preview and inbox type sizes are intentional.

### SURF-12 — Icon-only controls and technical terms get hints via tooltip, title, popover or nothing

low · visible · divergent-implementations · adjusted

- Tooltip: only three product sites use ui/tooltip (plus sidebar); each wraps its own TooltipProvider with delayDuration 0. — [reply-composer-footer.tsx:127-162](../../../src/components/inbox/reply-composer-footer.tsx#L127), [nav-items-shared.tsx:123-134](../../../src/components/layout/nav-items-shared.tsx#L123), [google-performance-section.tsx:126-144](../../../src/components/features/property/google-performance-section.tsx#L126), [sidebar.tsx:126](../../../src/components/ui/sidebar.tsx#L126)
- title attribute / aria-label only: about 30 icon-button call sites; the theme toggle and sidebar trigger use title, nearly all others give only aria-label, so sighted users get no hint. Timestamps use title across nine components. — [theme-toggle.tsx:29](../../../src/components/layout/theme-toggle.tsx#L29), [sidebar.tsx:287](../../../src/components/ui/sidebar.tsx#L287), [inbox-list-header.tsx:129](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-list-header.tsx#L129), [notification-row-menu.tsx:60](../../../src/components/features/notification/notification-row-menu.tsx#L60), [portal-overview-row-actions.tsx:127](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-row-actions.tsx#L127), [linktree-move-controls.tsx:46](../../../src/components/features/portal/link-tree/linktree-move-controls.tsx#L46)
- Dotted-underline explain-trigger copied: Popover with dotted underline is hand-copied in the glossary, Google source details and inbox due chip with different focus rings and widths; the same cue is used with a title attribute on withheld figures. — [glossary-term.tsx:24-29](../../../src/components/features/shared/glossary-term.tsx#L24), [google-performance-report.tsx:117-125](../../../src/components/features/property/google-performance-report.tsx#L117), [inbox-case-toolbar.tsx:322-343](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-case-toolbar.tsx#L322), [portal-overview-measure-cells.tsx:48-54](../../../src/components/features/portal/portal-overview/portal-overview-measure-cells.tsx#L48)
- Kbd: four callers with local overrides; beta feedback prints a hard-coded Command symbol though its handler accepts Ctrl as well. Shortcut help exists only for the Inbox. — [beta-feedback-form.tsx:84](../../../src/components/features/beta-feedback/beta-feedback-form.tsx#L84), [beta-feedback-form.tsx:215](../../../src/components/features/beta-feedback/beta-feedback-form.tsx#L215), [inbox-shortcuts-dialog.tsx:30](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-shortcuts-dialog.tsx#L30), [inbox-queue-rail.tsx:114](../../../src/components/inbox/inbox-queue-rail.tsx#L114)

**Why it matters:** Equivalent hints are discoverable in some places and invisible in others, and touch users get none from title.

**Could be intentional:** Glossary uses Popover on purpose (keyboard/phone reachable); the title-only and icon-button gaps look accidental.

**Recommended canonical:** ui/tooltip via one app-level TooltipProvider for icon buttons (an IconButton wrapper with label), and a shared ExplainTerm popover trigger.

**Verifier:** TooltipProvider appears in only a few files, consistent with the claim. The Kbd symbol point is minor and the glossary Popover is deliberate. Keep as low.

## Deliberate differences

Entries the verifier judged intentional (different task, scope or responsive adaptation); listed so they are not re-opened as defects. None counts toward the kept totals.

- FRAME-K1 — The two settings hubs own their page header differently (per-page header vs one layout header), with different shell, sidebar and spacing. Account/org settings (different sidebar, sectioned pages) vs Property settings (hub with in-page nav) differ in scope and task; the owner accepts different layouts for different tasks and the prior audit lists this as 'remain different'. Not a clear inconsistency. (low)
- NAV-K1 — Inbox queue switching has two anatomies and a rail heading unlike every other nav label. Rail vs strip is a responsive density adaptation; the strip's missing hover/focus is already covered by NAV-05; Button vs Link for a search-param queue is a design choice. (low)
- COLL-K1 — Bulk selection UI differs between Inbox and Google import. Feed with a hard bulk limit vs wizard-step selection; the differences follow task. (low)
- FORM-K1 — Adding an item to a list uses a different location, variant and icon convention on each page. Placement follows list context (page header vs card vs empty state) and dialog vs page follows entity size; only Plus icon convention drift is real and trivial. (low)
- SURF-K1 — Card-like surfaces split between ui/card and bespoke panels with different radius, padding, shadow and section anatomy. Mostly row tiles, composer docks and dense Portal workspace panels, which the owner accepts for different tasks; only the settings Card header/footer ruling inconsistency is a true same-function difference (narrow to that). (low)
- SURF-K2 — Sheets and popovers rebuild headers, widths and indicators per use. Popover on desktop and sheet on phone is a legitimate responsive split; only header anatomy and the copied count-pill class string are drift, a small cleanup. (low)

## Scope limits

Each dimension was a static source reading only; nothing was rendered, run or measured. Visual and cascade claims (accent link colour, red-on-red destructive label, top-bar height change, gutter shift, Sonner dark mode, margin collapse of mt-6 wrappers, dialog clipping, drawer staying open, duplicate aria-current) are inferred from CSS, Tailwind semantics and token values, not observed. Usage counts come from grep or ripgrep over src (excluding stories and tests) and may miss dynamically composed class strings or be off by a few. The guest portal (src/routes/p/$token.tsx, features/guest) was excluded. Storybook stories and e2e specs were generally not consulted. Areas explicitly not examined by a dimension are noted in its scope: auth routes, the Inbox detail pane and thread, Portal editor and link-tree internals, settings card anatomy beyond buttons and banners, and prior audit sections not cross-referenced above.
